# DESIGN-SYSTEM-CONSISTENCY

**Status:** DECOMPOSED — the executable work now lives in [`../atomic/DSC.md`](../atomic/DSC.md) as 12 atomic plan(s).

This plan was split because parts of it blocked on other plans, which forced it to sit half-done while other work ran. Each atom below its own file executes start-to-finish in one go; the dependency graph lives in [`../atomic/dag.json`](../atomic/dag.json).

The original design record is kept below — execution logs, measured findings and owner rulings are the reason this document still matters.

# Plan: Design-System Consistency — One Coherent Surface, Everywhere

**Status:** DONE — closed by owner 2026-07-24 (created 2026-07-18, roadmap rev 10; owner ask: UI/UX consistencies). S1 audit + S2 token/primitive hardening + S3 interaction/a11y/responsive parity all landed across cy1–cy18 (see `## Execution log`); WCAG AA reached on all 11 schemes in both modes with a permanent per-scheme contrast guard; every byte-identical convergence cluster consolidated onto shared primitives; the primitive-adoption + inline-font-weight ratchets and the strict token-lint are live and block regression. **Owner-closed with a known, honestly-recorded tail** (not silently dropped): the authenticated axe-per-route CI gate (T3.2/T3.4) and the V3 full-app keyboard/reduced-motion/phone walkthrough need a seeded, authenticated, per-route CI harness that was not stood up here; the `ErrorState` primitive (single faithful adopter) and the harness-gated `CodeCockpitPage`-class raw-button *redesign* are deferred as owner/design decisions, not consistency work. Ratchets keep the surface from drifting back; any of the deferred items can be picked up as follow-on plans. **Tail update 2026-08-18 — `DSC-12` DONE, so the OWNER/DESIGN half of that tail is closed:** the load-error twin is folded onto `ui/ListScaffold`’s `LoadError` (the "single faithful adopter" objection was overtaken by events — the primitive shipped anyway and had thirteen adopters by the time this was picked up), and all 21 `CodeCockpitPage` raw `<button>`s are classified: 7 migrated onto `Button`/`IconButton`/`TextLink`, 14 excluded with a recorded reason each (`rawButton` ratchet 272 → 265). Only the **environment-blocked** half remains — `DSC-11` (authenticated seeded per-route axe CI gate T3.2/T3.4 + the V3 full-app walkthrough), still genuinely blocked on a harness that does not exist.
🟢 **The harness that tail was waiting for now has an owner (2026-08-04):**
[PLATFORM-HARDENING-FLOORS](PLATFORM-HARDENING-FLOORS.md) §4 builds the offline **fake-model E2E
harness** — a real gateway booted against a packaged fake model provider with a seeded
authenticated session, then Playwright shelled at it (no model, no credentials, no network, no
cost) — and `SH4.3` explicitly mounts this plan's `T3.2`/`T3.4` axe-per-route a11y rail on it. So
T3.2/T3.4/V3 are **DEFERRED with a named owner**, not open-ended.
**Created:** 2026-07-18
**Wave:** 2 — a consistency-hardening pass that every other product-surface plan then inherits; run it early so new work lands on a clean baseline.
**Depends on:** nothing hard (audits + hardens the shipped design system). Feeds FLUID-MOTION (52 — consistent components are the substrate motion animates), APP-PLATFORM-EVOLUTION (48 — apps consume the exported primitives), and every page-touching plan (43, 49, 50).
**Scope:** the design system is mature (tokens, `tokenRegistry`, a token-lint test, 41 shell primitives, `web/PRODUCT.md` + `web/DESIGN.md`) — but 20+ pages built over time drift from it. This plan is a **consistency audit + hardening**: verify every surface uses tokens (not hardcoded values), the shell primitives (not bespoke chrome), consistent interaction patterns, WCAG AA parity, and dark/light + responsive parity — then ratchet the lint so drift can't return. **Soul guardrail:** this is *consistency*, not redesign — no new visual language, no page rewrites; bring outliers to the existing system (`DESIGN.md`/`PRODUCT.md` are authority). Where a page genuinely needs a pattern the system lacks, add it to the system *once* (a new shared primitive), never a one-off.

---

## Context (code recon, 2026-07-18)

Strong foundation: `web/src/design/` — `tokens.css` (brand/surface/content/semantic/glow/typography/layout/shape/elevation/3D/motion token groups), `tokenRegistry.ts` (typed `Token` union, 11 `GROUPS`), `tokenLint.test.ts` + `tokenLint.allowlist.json` (**a linter already exists** — the ratchet target), `schemes.ts` (light/dark), `runtime.ts` (canvas glow), `motion.ts`, `gradients.ts`. Shell primitives (41 in `web/src/ui/`): TopBar, ListScaffold, SidePanel, HeaderActions, NavRail, Modal, Surface, Button, IconButton, Toggle, Segmented, Combobox, Popover, Toaster, WorkbenchLayout, etc. `PRODUCT.md` sets the doctrine ("companion not console," earned familiarity, everything-is-a-token, WCAG AA). `web/src/ui/cx.ts` (class util).

**Where drift lives (the audit will quantify):** the two big pages (`ChatPage.tsx` 3384 LOC, `CodeCockpitPage.tsx` 3386 LOC, loop cockpits ~1000+ LOC each) predate later primitives and likely carry bespoke chrome + hardcoded values the allowlist tolerates; empty states vary (plan 43/49 add `EmptyState`); the two first-party UI apps (Minutes/Growth) don't use the system at all (plan 48 migrates them); dark/light parity and focus-visible/reduced-motion coverage are per-component, not audited system-wide.

## Design

- **S1 — The audit (measure before touching):** an automated inventory — extend the token-lint to *report* (not just fail on allowlist) every hardcoded color/spacing/radius/shadow/duration in `web/src`, grouped by file; a primitive-adoption scan (pages using bespoke chrome vs shell primitives); a WCAG scan (axe over each route) and a reduced-motion/focus-visible coverage check. Output: `docs/design/consistency-audit.md` — a ranked drift list. **No fixes in S1** — this is the map.
- **S2 — Token + primitive hardening:** fix the ranked drift — replace hardcoded values with tokens (shrinking the allowlist as you go), migrate bespoke chrome to shell primitives, consolidate near-duplicate components (e.g. multiple ad-hoc button/menu variants → the canonical primitive). Each fix shrinks the lint allowlist; the end state = allowlist near-empty, lint strict.
- **S3 — Interaction + a11y + responsive parity:** standardize interaction patterns (list selection, empty states via the shared `EmptyState`, loading skeletons, error states, confirm dialogs — one pattern each, documented in a `docs/design/patterns.md` gallery); WCAG AA parity pass (every route: contrast, focus-visible ring, keyboard nav, reduced-motion alternative); dark/light parity pass (every token used in both, no theme-only hardcodes); responsive pass (no horizontal body scroll; the phone-viewport paths for plan 44's companion). Ratchet: the lint + an a11y CI check now *block* regressions (feeds plan 33 rails).

## Contracts & Interfaces (mostly frontend hardening; the enforceable rails pinned — conventions per [AGENTS.md](../../../AGENTS.md))

### C1 — The consistency rails (CI-enforced, mount in plan 33 `ci.yml` web/rails)
- **Token lint (strict):** `web/src/design/tokenLint.test.ts` extended — every hardcoded design value in `web/src` must be a token or an explicit, justified allowlist entry (`tokenLint.allowlist.json` with a `reason` field per entry). Ratchets down each session; end state near-empty.
- **A11y check:** axe-core over each route (headless) in web CI — no serious/critical violations; reduced-motion + focus-visible asserted.
- **Primitive-adoption check:** a lint that flags bespoke chrome elements (raw `<button>`, ad-hoc modals) outside `web/src/ui/` primitives, allowlisted with reasons.

### C2 — Pattern gallery (`docs/design/patterns.md` + a Storybook-lite route, optional)
Canonical usage of each shared primitive + each interaction pattern (selection, empty, loading, error, confirm). This is the "how to stay consistent" reference every page-touching plan cites.

### Integration points
- **Touches:** every `web/src/pages/*` surface (consistency fixes) + `web/src/ui/*` (consolidation) + `web/src/design/*` (token/lint hardening).
- **Consumed by:** 52 (motion animates consistent components), 48 (apps import the hardened primitives via UI SDK), 43/49/50 (new surfaces inherit the patterns + `EmptyState`).
- **Rails into:** plan 33 CI (`web`/`rails` jobs gain the token-lint-strict + axe checks).
- **Authority:** `web/DESIGN.md` + `web/PRODUCT.md` (unchanged — this plan conforms TO them).

## Task breakdown (executor-ready — run under the roadmap session discipline in [AGENTS.md](../../../AGENTS.md))

### Session 1 — The audit (map only)

| T1.2 | Primitive-adoption + a11y + parity scan → the ranked audit doc | `docs/design/consistency-audit.md`, scan scripts | audit ranks the worst offenders (likely ChatPage/CodeCockpit/loop cockpits); each entry actionable |

| ID | Task | Files | Done when |
|---|---|---|---|
| T2.1 | Fix the top-ranked drift: hardcoded values → tokens; shrink the allowlist (each removed entry = a real fix) | the ranked pages, `tokenLint.allowlist.json` | allowlist materially smaller; those pages token-clean; visual diff shows no regression (screenshot compare) |
| T2.2 | Migrate bespoke chrome → shell primitives; consolidate duplicate components into the canonical primitive | outlier pages, `web/src/ui/` | duplicates removed; pages use shared primitives; suite + build green |
| T2.3 | Add any genuinely-missing primitive to the system ONCE (if the audit found a real gap) with a pattern-gallery entry | `web/src/ui/`, `docs/design/patterns.md` | new primitive documented + adopted where needed; not a one-off |
| V2 | Validation: the hardened pages look identical-or-better; token-lint allowlist near-empty for them | — | holds |

### Session 3 — Interaction + a11y + responsive parity

| ID | Task | Files | Done when |
|---|---|---|---|
| T3.1 | Standardize interaction patterns (selection/empty/loading/error/confirm) across pages via shared primitives; document each in `patterns.md` | pages, `docs/design/patterns.md` | each pattern has one implementation + one doc entry; pages conform |
| T3.2 | WCAG AA parity pass: axe over every route → fix serious/criticals; focus-visible + keyboard nav + reduced-motion alternatives everywhere | pages, `ui/` | axe clean on all routes; keyboard-only walkthrough of the app works |
| T3.3 | Dark/light + responsive parity: every token used in both themes (no theme-only hardcodes); no horizontal body scroll at phone widths | pages, tokens | theme toggle on every page shows no broken surface; phone-width pass clean |
| T3.4 | Ratchet: mount token-lint-strict + axe into plan 33 web/rails CI (block regressions) | `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (coordinate with plan 33) | a new hardcoded value or a11y regression turns CI red |
| V3 | Validation: full app walkthrough in both themes, keyboard-only, reduced-motion, and a phone viewport — consistent throughout | — | holds |

## Owner tasks (real world)
1. **Review the audit** (S1 output) and confirm the priority order — you may weight the surfaces you use most.
2. **Taste sign-off on any consolidation** where two patterns merge into one (e.g. if two menu styles become one) — that's a visual-identity call.
3. Confirm the WCAG target stays **AA** (PRODUCT.md says AA) — AAA is not proposed.

## Risks & open questions
- **"Consistency" creeping into "redesign"** — the soul guardrail forbids it; every S2/S3 change is verified by screenshot-diff to show no unintended visual change (only conformance). If a fix *requires* a visual change, that's an owner taste call (task 2), not silent scope.
- **The two mega-pages** (ChatPage/CodeCockpit) are the highest-effort, highest-risk fixes — do them in small, screenshot-verified increments; a full rewrite is explicitly out of scope (would be its own plan).
- **Open:** Storybook-lite route vs a static pattern doc — default: static `patterns.md` (zero new dep); add a live gallery route only if the team grows (ratchet).

## Execution log

### 2026-07-22 — S1 cycle 1 (audit reporter + drift map)
- **DONE (T1.1):** Built the drift reporter — `web/src/design/consistencyAudit.report.ts` (`scanDrift()` inventories color/spacing/radius/shadow/duration + a primitive-adoption scan, grouped by file:line; `rankFiles()` weighted worst-first) and `web/src/design/consistencyAudit.test.ts` (runs the scan, emits `docs/design/consistency-audit.json`; measure-only, never fails on drift). Zero new deps — mirrors the existing `tokenLint.test.ts` pattern, runs under `npm test`.
- **DONE (T1.2):** Authored `docs/design/consistency-audit.md` — the ranked drift map from measured data. V1 spot-check: 5 findings verified against code (all hold).
- **DISCOVERY:** Token layer is already near-clean (**7 raw-value hits total**, most legitimate: SVG drag durations, generated HSL graph palette, CodeMirror `0.9em`, one `calc(-36px)` widget offset). The DOMINANT drift is **primitive adoption**: **420 raw `<button>` + 206 raw form elements across 112 files**, topped by the two mega-pages (CodeCockpit 51, ChatPage 34). `Modal` is already the sole canonical dialog (0 bespoke). → S2's payload is primitive consolidation, not token cleanup.
- **DISCOVERY:** There is **no shared form-field primitive** — the one genuine system gap (T2.3): a `TextField`/`Field` in `web/src/ui/` + `patterns.md` entry, to land before migrating the 206 raw inputs.
- **BLOCKED (S2 prerequisite):** the Playwright visual-regression harness is not yet built (`web/package.json` has only `vitest`; no `@playwright/test`/`axe-core`). Per the brief this harness must exist and capture baselines BEFORE any S2 fix. Next cycle: stand it up.
- **Verification:** `cd web && npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `npx vitest run` 72/72 green (+2 from the reporter). **No fixes made this session** (S1 is map-only).

### 2026-07-22 — S1 cycle 2 (a11y static coverage scan)
- **DONE (T1.2, a11y portion):** Extended the reporter with `scanA11y()` (reduced-motion + focus-visible coverage) and wired it into the JSON + audit doc. Replaced the "pending" a11y placeholder with measured coverage.
- **DISCOVERY:** a11y coverage is **global-by-default, not per-component** — `tokens.css` has a `*` reduced-motion rule AND a global `:focus-visible` ring, and `App.tsx` wraps everything in Framer `<MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">`. So the 144 animated files + 171 `outline-none` occurrences (70 files) are all covered by the global nets — NOT violations to fix individually (fixing them = churn). The S3 ratchet must PROTECT the two global rules (assertion now in consistencyAudit.test.ts) and add dynamic axe-per-route (needs harness).
- **Verification:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `npx vitest run` 72/72 green. Static a11y scan complete; dynamic axe-per-route + keyboard walkthrough remain BLOCKED on the Playwright harness (next cycle). No fixes made (S1 map-only).

- **DONE (S2 hard prerequisite — infrastructure):** Built the visual-regression + a11y harness in `web/` mirroring the personalclaw.dev pattern — `playwright.config.ts` (platform-qualified `toHaveScreenshot` baselines, `maxDiffPixelRatio` tolerance, animations disabled, vite-preview webServer), `e2e/routes.ts` (route SoT), `e2e/helpers.ts` (theme seed + settle), `e2e/visual.spec.ts` (16 routes × 2 themes), `e2e/a11y.spec.ts` (axe wcag2aa, fail on serious/critical), `e2e/README.md`, npm scripts (`e2e`, `e2e:visual`, `e2e:a11y`, `e2e:update`, `e2e:report`). Added `@playwright/test` + `@axe-core/playwright` devDeps; `npx playwright install chromium` succeeds; gitignored run artifacts.

A fresh unauthenticated Playwright context (both vs `vite preview` and vs the live gateway :10000) renders a **blank body** → a naive capture produced 32 *identical blank* PNGs. **Those were discarded, NOT committed** (a false safety rail is worse than none). Recorded the auth-seed default (owner/CI runs `e2e:update` against an authenticated session; CI mints a test token via a global-setup stub).

My earlier same-cycle "32/32 clean / zero-diff" reading was against the blank app and is INVALID — corrected here.

- **NEXT:** land the auth-seed (`e2e/auth.setup.ts` + storageState) so S2 visual verification is trustworthy — this is now the true S2 gate, ahead of the first primitive-consolidation fix.

### 2026-07-22 — S2 cycle 4 (first consolidation: notification unread-rail + tone-chip helpers)
- **DONE (T2.2, pure-conformance consolidation — no visual change):** the verbatim-duplicated unread accent rail (`boxShadow: inset 2px 0 0 0 ${tone}`) and tinted icon-chip background (`color-mix(in srgb, ${tone} 16%, transparent)`) lived in both `NotificationsPage.tsx` (Row, 3 sites) and `NotificationBell.tsx` (ShadeRow, 2 sites). Extracted to `unreadRail(tone, acked)` + `toneChipBg(tone)` in `notificationMeta.ts` (the module both already import). Output is byte-identical → zero visual change; safe to make without the (auth-blocked) visual harness.
- **Guard:** `notificationMeta.test.ts` pins the exact helper output (inset rail string, 16% tint string, token-routed tones) so the consolidation can't silently drift.
- **Measured effect:** the audit reporter's shadow-drift count dropped 2 → 1 (the single canonical helper is the only remaining `inset …px` source). Duplication eliminated.
- **Evidence:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `npx vitest run` **75/75** green (72 + 3 new guard tests). Chose this over the `WidgetFrame calc(-36px)` fix (DEFERRED — that 36px is a sibling-toolbar height, a layout coupling, not a scale value; changing it needs visual verification which is auth-blocked).
- Because the visual harness is dormant (auth blocker), I am prioritizing consolidations that are byte-identical by construction (provably zero visual change) — the brief's "proceed on all pure-conformance fixes" — and deferring anything that could shift pixels until baselines can be captured.

- **DISCOVERY:** the name `EmptyState` was used for TWO genuinely different components — the full-height centered page-empty (`ui/ListScaffold`) and a deliberately compact dashboard-slot strip (`pages/dashboard/widgets/kit.tsx`, whose own comment justifies why it's NOT centered). ### 2026-07-22 — S2 cycle 8 (fvs() weight helper + .fw-* utilities — canonicalize inline font weights)
- **DISCOVERY (audit deepening):** `font-variation-settings: "wght" <n>` is set INLINE ~180 times across pages (75× 500, 48× 600, 42× 550, 11× 470, 6× 400, 1× 650) — the single largest repeated inline-style pattern, with no shared home.
- **DONE (additive, zero-pixel):** added `web/src/design/fontWeight.ts` (`fvs(n)` → the identical `{ fontVariationSettings: '"wght" <n>' }`, plus `withWeight()`) and matching `.fw-400/470/500/550/600/650` utility classes in `tokens.css`. Both emit the SAME output the call sites hand-write — byte-identical drop-ins. Documented in `patterns.md` (prefer a type-role when the element maps to one; use fvs()/.fw-* for weight-only nudges).
- **DONE (proof migration):** converted the 2 sites in `NotificationsPage.tsx` to `fvs(500)` (byte-identical). ~180 remain — incremental, each a provable zero-pixel swap; safe to grind down without the visual harness.
- **Guard:** `fontWeight.test.ts` pins `fvs()`/`withWeight()` output.
- **Evidence:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `npx vitest run` **88/88** green (+2); `npm run build` green (CSS utilities compile).

### 2026-07-22 — S3 cycle 12 (T3.1/C2: document the remaining interaction patterns)

### 2026-07-23 — S2 cycle 17 (fix a density-doctrine bug in the TextField primitive + correct the audit premise)
- **VISUAL VERIFICATION (the cycle-16 waves, now confirmed):** brought up the tokenless dev gateway (`AUTH_MODE=none`, seeded empty, `user_name` set — cycle 14 recipe) and ran the full harness against the built SPA on the branch tip. **visual 32/32 + axe 32/32 green** → the 3 post-baseline commits (CodeCockpit/ChatPage button waves, empty-state consolidation) produced ZERO unintended pixel diff and no a11y regression. The safety rail confirms "consistency not redesign" held.
- **DISCOVERY (latent defect in the cycle-5 primitive):** `ui/TextField`/`TextArea` hardcoded a fixed `px-3`/`py-2`. But density is a LIVE user setting — `appearance.tsx` sets `data-ui="dense"|"cli"` on `<html>`, re-scaling `--space-scale` (1 → 0.8 → 0.68), and the system's explicit doctrine is *"no per-component density px — density falls out app-wide for free"* (tokens.css P19). A fixed `px-3` would make the field STOP tightening at dense/cli, and since `px-m` is the majority padding in the raw inputs it was built to replace (232 `px-m` vs 148 `px-3` app-wide), every future migration off it would silently regress density. Caught before adoption (the primitive is imported nowhere yet).
- **DONE (fix):** `px-3`→`px-m`, `py-2`→`py-s` in `textFieldClass`/`AREA_BASE` (leading-icon `pr-3`→`pr-m`; `pl-9` stays — it clears the fixed-position icon). `px-m == 12px == px-3` at the default *comfortable* density, so this is **byte-identical there** (proven: visual 32/32 unchanged) while correctly tightening at dense/cli. Updated `TextField.test.tsx` contract (px-m) + the primitive's doc comment.
- **CORRECTION (audit premise):** the S1 audit's "**no** shared form-field primitive" was wrong — `pages/tasks/formControls.tsx` already exports an adopted, density-correct, a11y-wired `Field`/`TextInput`/`TextArea`/`Select` family (tasks/schedule/inbox import it). Recorded in the `ui/TextField` header. Two form families is itself a consistency defect, BUT collapsing them is a "two patterns → one" merge (different radius/size) = an **OWNER TASTE call** (plan Owner task 2) — flagged, not silently forced.
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean; vitest **89/89**; build green; visual **32/32**; axe **32/32** (all against the live gateway).
- **OPEN (owner taste, queued):** (1) merge `ui/TextField` ↔ `formControls` form family into one home (a visual-identity call — pixels shift on one call-site set); (2) the 206 raw-input → shared-primitive migration is design-unblocked now the primitive is density-correct, but each swap is pixel-verified. Continuing byte-identical/zero-diff work meanwhile.

So the "risky two-patterns merge" is actually a **clean-break dead-code removal**: keep the adopted family, delete the unused primitive. No new visual language; consistency-not-redesign preserved — in fact the competing shape is *removed*.
- **DONE (clean break):** `git rm web/src/ui/TextField.tsx web/src/ui/TextField.test.tsx` (primitive + its class-contract test — the test only asserted the deleted helpers' class strings, so it dies with the file). Baseline stays `{ rawButton: 377, rawInput: 206, rawDialog: 0, inlineFontWeight: 7 }`.

Verified no external consumer imports `useFieldLabelId`/`FieldLabelCtx` (only the module itself), and every `Segmented`/`SegOption` direct import elsewhere already points at `ui/Segmented` (untouched). `AgentDetail.tsx`'s `RoutingNotesEditor` was a substring false-positive, not an importer.

Classified **intentional-existing, left unchanged**: they are byte-identical copies of shipped code, this cycle is a pure relocation (normalizing them would move Playwright baselines — forbidden here — and is a redesign that must apply uniformly across all form surfaces, not smuggled into a file-move). ### 2026-07-23 — S2 cycle 30 (T2.3: codify the standard-field size/surface scale)
- **Context (owner directive):** owner asked for "the right thing by the system — a correct combination of option 2 (standardize input sizes: canonical field-height scale, family variants, accepting deliberate pixel changes for off-scale outliers) and option 3 (grow the family API: type/onBlur/disabled/leadingIcon)". Soul guardrail held: **consistency, not redesign.**
- **DONE (T2.3 — the scale, byte-identical):** `TextInput` now carries a principled **size** (sm/md/lg → h-8/h-9/h-10) × **surface** (container/high/base) scale via `FIELD_SIZE`/`FIELD_SURFACE` tables + a shared `INPUT_BASE` invariant-chrome const. Defaults (lg/container/0.9375rem) reproduce the prior fixed chrome **token-for-token** — verified by `forms.test.tsx` render assertions (7 tests: byte-identical defaults, on-ramp size steps with a negative guard that no size emits the off-ramp `text-[0.875rem]`, surface-swaps-only-fill, TextArea/Select default chrome). Every size uses a DESIGN.md-blessed type size (0.8125rem/0.9375rem); 0.875rem/14px stays off-ramp drift, never a tier.
- **DONE (adopter):** the one byte-identical raw input — `AgentForm` CheckList search — migrated to `TextInput size="sm" surface="base"`. Ratchet `rawInput` **201→200** in lockstep.
- **DELIBERATE SCOPE CORRECTION (clean-break tenet):** an initial pass of this cycle also grew the *behavioral/structural* API (`type/onBlur/disabled/inputMode/min/max/step/maxLength/spellCheck/required/ref/leadingIcon`) ahead of adopters. A full audit of **all 139 raw inputs** falsified that: (a) the numeric-stepper props (`min/max/step/inputMode`) serve only `type=number` fields, and **every** stepper (ScheduleForm/ChatPanel/MemoryPanel/InboxSettingsPanel ×2) diverges on **width** (`w-24`/`w-28`/`w-20` vs the primitive's `w-full`) + `text-right`/`tabular-nums` — a distinct compact role a full-width field cannot render, so those props are **permanently dead here**; (b) the owner-requested props (`type`/`onBlur`/`disabled`/`leadingIcon`) have **no same-commit adopter** — their honest adopters (ToolsPage `type=password`+mono fields; the compact-search cluster `leadingIcon` via a reviewed 1px geometry snap) land in the follow-on cluster migrations. Per **clean break** (no API a caller doesn't yet use), the props were trimmed; `TextArea`/`DateInput`/`Select` reverted to their exact pre-scale bodies. They + each behavioral prop **grow in lockstep with the first real adopter, never ahead of one** — so option 3 lands *correctly* (adopter-backed) rather than speculatively. End state the owner asked for; only commit sequencing changed so no commit ships dead API.
- **Design-hook (impeccable) findings:** `forms.tsx` L172 (`text-[0.875rem]`, ChipInput draft field) is **pre-existing** drift (byte-identical to commit `1c76149`, shifted line only because the trim shrank the file) — classified intentional-existing, unchanged (T2.1 normalization, separate + uniform). `forms.test.tsx` L53/L57 are **test-assertion string literals** (the on-ramp expectation + the negative off-ramp guard), not applied styles — the mechanism that *enforces* the ramp; intentional false positives, unchanged. No suppressions added (await explicit owner sign-off).
- **Zero pixels:** default `TextInput` render is token-for-token the pre-scale field (the trim even dropped Phase-A's inert `disabled:opacity-50`, restoring exact parity); `AgentForm` search migration byte-identical at rest. Confirmed.
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean; vitest **87/87** (ratchet green at 200; −2 vs prior 89 = the two dead-API tests removed with the trim); build green; visual **64/64** (all routes byte-identical, no baseline moved). `consistency-audit.json` timestamp-only churn reverted (no count delta — no raw-input site changed beyond the already-counted AgentForm migration).

- **NEXT (cluster migrations):** grow `leadingIcon` in lockstep with the compact-search cluster (ArchivePanel/AuditPanel/MemoryPanel — bare leading-icon filters), a **deliberate reviewed pixel snap** (`left-2.5→left-3`, `pl-8→pl-9`) — owner review required (pixels move). Byte-identical adopters that need no new prop (ToolsPage mcp fields → `size="md" surface="high"`, once a `mono` prop or equivalent lands with its first `font-mono` adopter) proceed autonomously. Compound searches (ModelsPanel/TasksListPage: trailing clear-button + `type=search`) and numeric steppers stay distinct roles for dedicated primitives, not forced onto the standard field.

### 2026-07-23 — S2 cycle 31 (cluster migration 1: ToolsPage add-server modal → the scale) — owner-authorized density fix
- **Context (owner directive, "Proceed — treat as intended fixes"):** the cycle-30 density audit established that the primitive's `INPUT_BASE` uses density-aware `px-m` (`= calc(12px * var(--space-scale))`) while the hand-rolled ToolsPage inputs used fixed `px-3` (`= 12px`). `px-m == px-3` **only at comfortable density** — at dense/cli the primitive tightens and the raw field does not. So no remaining raw-input cluster is a truly free byte-identical swap; every migration moves pixels at dense/cli. Owner reviewed this and authorized proceeding, treating the density-correction as a deliberate improvement (updating Playwright baselines where pixels legitimately move; growing behavioral props in lockstep with adopters).
- **DONE (T2.3 adopter — first cluster):** migrated the **7** raw `<input>` in `AddToolServerModal` (`pages/tools/ToolsPage.tsx`) — mcp branch (name/command/args) + openai branch (name/endpoint/apiKey/toolFilter) — to `<TextInput size="md" surface="high" [mono] [type="password"]>`. The `mcpInputCls` shape (`h-9` + `bg-surface-high` + `px-3` + `text-[0.8125rem]` + shared focus/placeholder chrome) maps token-for-token onto `size="md"` (`h-9 text-[0.8125rem]`) × `surface="high"` (`bg-surface-high`), with `px-3`→`px-m` the sole authorized density-correction. The env `<textarea>` (distinct `min-h-16 py-2` shape) stays raw; `mcpInputCls` remains for it.
- **DONE (option 3, adopter-backed prop growth — never ahead of an adopter):** grew `TextInput` with exactly the two props these 7 fields need — `type?: 'text' | 'password'` (the API-Key field masks its secret) and `mono?: boolean` (command/args/endpoint/apiKey/toolFilter are `font-mono` technical values, mirroring `TextArea`'s existing `mono`). Both are honest, same-commit-adopted; no speculative API. Default `type` stays absent (byte-identical to the pre-scale field, which set none) — locked by `forms.test.tsx`'s `getAttribute('type')).toBeNull()` assertion.
- **Ratchet DOWN (live-measured via `scanDrift()`, not guessed):** `rawInput` **200→193** (−7) in lockstep in the same commit; `rawButton` (376) / `rawDialog` (0) / `inlineFontWeight` (7) unchanged. `consistency-audit.json` regenerated: ToolsPage raw-input **8→1** (the 1 remaining is the deliberately-left env textarea), score 24→17; `generatedAt` timestamp rides along with the real count delta.
- **a11y note (unchanged):** ToolsPage has a LOCAL bespoke `Field` (its own duplicate, not the canonical `ui/forms` one) that publishes **no** `FieldLabelCtx`, so `TextInput` inside it emits no `aria-labelledby` — byte-identical accessible-name behavior to the raw inputs it replaced (each was label-associated only visually). The generated `id` is unreferenced/non-visual. Consolidating ToolsPage's local `Field` onto the canonical one is a separate future task, not smuggled in here.

The visual harness runs at comfortable, so it stays **64/64 green with no baseline change** — the authorized pixel move is only observable at dense/cli, which the harness does not yet snapshot. Flagged for owner review of dense/cli screenshots.
- **Design-hook (impeccable):** the shifting-line re-flags of pre-existing off-ramp literals (`forms.tsx` ChipInput `text-[0.875rem]`; `forms.test.tsx` L53/L57 test-assertion literals; ToolsPage's own pre-existing `text-[…]` findings) are all classified pre-existing/intentional as in cycle 30 — no suppressions added (await explicit owner sign-off).
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean; vitest **87/87** (ratchet green at 193); build green; visual **64/64** (comfortable-density byte-identical, no baseline moved). Gateway live on `127.0.0.1:10400` for review.

### 2026-07-23 — S2 cycle 32 (cluster migration 2: the bare leading-icon search fields → the scale) — owner-authorized geometry decision
- **Context (owner decision via AskUserQuestion):** the audit premise from cycle 31 ("3 outliers snapping onto a single canonical geometry") was FALSIFIED on full inspection — the app shipped **two** distinct leading-icon search geometries, not one: `left-2.5`/`pl-8` (the plurality, ~8 fields, mostly in un-snapshotted Settings sub-panels) vs `left-3`/`pl-9` (~6 fields). Rather than proceed on the stale premise I surfaced the two-geometry reality to the owner. **Owner blessed `left-3`/`pl-9`** — the geometry the deleted `ui/TextField` primitive had *defined* but which never shipped (0 adopters) — as canonical. Consequence the owner explicitly accepted: the `left-2.5`/`pl-8` fields snap OUT by ~1px, and most live in Settings sub-panels the top-level route harness does not snapshot, so there are few reviewable snapshots for the movers.
- **DONE (option 3, adopter-backed prop growth):** grew `TextInput` with exactly one new prop — `leadingIcon?: ReactNode` — replicating the deleted primitive's exact geometry: a `pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-low` icon span wrapping the input, with the input taking `pl-9 pr-m` (clears the fixed `left-3` glyph; keeps the canonical density-aware right pad). `INPUT_BASE` lost its baked-in `px-m` so padding applies conditionally (`leadingIcon ? 'pl-9 pr-m' : 'px-m'`) — the two never both emit, avoiding a `padding-inline`+`padding-left` cascade race. This is *consistency, not redesign*: the shape already existed in-tree.
- **DONE (T2.3 adopters — 6 bare leading-icon filters):** migrated to `<TextInput … leadingIcon={<Search size={14} />}>`:
  - `settings/ArchivePanel.tsx` (`size="md" surface="high"`), `settings/AuditPanel.tsx` (`md`/`high`), `settings/MemoryPanel.tsx` **only the MemoryAuditPanel filter** (`md`/`high`) — the :272 studio search (off-ramp `text-[0.8rem]`) is HELD for a separate T2.1 text-normalization task, not smuggled in.
  - `workflows/WorkflowForm.tsx` WorkflowRefPicker (`sm`/`base`, `autoFocus`), `files/artifacts/ArtifactList.tsx` (`sm`/`high`), `tasks/formControls.tsx` DependencyEditor picker (`sm`/`base`, `autoFocus`, load-bearing random `name`).
- **a11y fix (root-caused, not worked around):** DependencyEditor's raw input emitted BOTH a random `name` (anti-autofill) AND `aria-label`. `TextInput`'s accessible-name logic was `aria-label={!labelId && !name ? ariaLabel : undefined}` — a `name` suppressed an explicit `ariaLabel`, silently dropping the accessible name (a `name` is NOT an accessible name). Fixed the shared logic to `claimsFieldLabel = !!labelId && !name` → `aria-labelledby={claimsFieldLabel ? labelId : undefined}` / `aria-label={claimsFieldLabel ? undefined : ariaLabel}`, so a `name` never suppresses `ariaLabel`. Verified byte-identical for every other caller (the `AppsSection` `name=` callers pass no `ariaLabel`; the `ariaLabel`-passing callers are all bare filters outside any `Field`, so `labelId` is undefined). Locked by a new `forms.test.tsx` assertion (`ariaLabel survives a name`).
- **Ratchet DOWN (live-measured via `scanDrift()`, not guessed):** `rawInput` **193→187** (−6, one per migrated field) in the same commit; `rawButton` (376) / `rawDialog` (0) / `inlineFontWeight` (7) unchanged. `consistency-audit.json` regenerated: exactly six raw-input decrements (ArchivePanel/AuditPanel/ArtifactList 1→0, MemoryPanel 9→8, formControls/WorkflowForm 3→2) — no collateral.
- **Deliberate pixel change (owner-authorized, low snapshot coverage):** converging onto `left-3`/`pl-9 pr-m` moves the `left-2.5`/`pl-8` movers ~1px at *all* densities (plus the usual `px`→`px-m` density behavior at dense/cli). All 6 movers live in interaction-gated pickers (WorkflowForm/DependencyEditor) or un-snapshotted Settings sub-panels + the Files artifacts rail, so the top-level route harness captures none of them — visual stays **64/64 green with no baseline change**. The owner explicitly accepted this low coverage when blessing the `left-3`/`pl-9` geometry.
- **Design-hook (impeccable):** `forms.test.tsx` L53/L57 (on-ramp assertion + `text-[0.875rem]` off-ramp *negative guard*) re-flagged as before — test-assertion literals that MUST stay verbatim for the scale test to red on drift; classified intentional, no suppressions added.
- **Validation finding (driven from the UI, then fixed):** opening the WorkflowForm ref-picker in the browser revealed its search reported its accessible name as **"Steps"** (inherited from the enclosing `<Field label="Steps">` via `aria-labelledby`), making the `ariaLabel="Find a workflow to add"` I passed **dead** — and inconsistent with its structurally-identical DependencyEditor sibling (which reads "Find a prerequisite task"). Root cause: the picker had no `name`, so `claimsFieldLabel` was true and the Field label won. Fix (mirrors DependencyEditor): give it a random `name={`wf-ref-search-${useId()}`}` — defeats autofill AND keeps its own label, honoring the Field's documented "multi-control Fields keep per-input labels" contract. This picker was the ONLY one of the 6 adopters nested inside a `Field`; the other 4 are bare filters where `ariaLabel` already wins. Behavioral-only (adds a `name` attr) — no primitive-count / pixel change.
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean; vitest **89/89** (+2: leadingIcon geometry + ariaLabel-survives-name; ratchet green at 187); build green; visual **64/64** (no baseline moved); UI-validated the Audit filter + both pickers on the live gateway (accessible names correct). Gateway live on `127.0.0.1:10400` for review.
- **Baseline now `{ rawButton: 376, rawInput: 187, rawDialog: 0, inlineFontWeight: 7 }`.**
- **NEXT (cluster 3):** the compound-search cluster (LocalModelManager `pr-8`, FilesSection `pr-7`, ChatPage/ModelsPanel/TasksListPage) — `type="search"` + a clear button is a distinct role; likely its own dedicated primitive that inherits the `left-3`/`pl-9` geometry rather than overloading `TextInput`. Then numeric steppers (ScheduleForm/ChatPanel/MemoryPanel/InboxSettingsPanel×2 — distinct role) and the off-ramp text-normalization (MemoryPanel:272 etc., T2.1).

### 2026-07-23 — cycle 33 (validation finding: settings bento inline switches were inert — interaction correctness fix)
- **Owner-reported bug (validate-as-a-user):** on the Settings home the bento cards expose inline controls (switches/segmented/selects) for direct interaction, but "the toggles are not interactable." Reproduced + root-caused rather than worked around.
- **ROOT CAUSE:** `pages/settings/bento.tsx` `Switch` wrapped the canonical `Toggle` in `<span … onClickCapture={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>`. `onClickCapture` fires in the **capture phase** (root→target); `stopPropagation()` there halted the click *before* it reached the inner `<button role="switch">`, so `Toggle`'s own `onClick` never fired — every inline switch on the settings cards was inert. The sibling controls `SegToggle` (L154) and `InlineSelect` (L171) already stop propagation in the **bubble phase** (plain `onClick` on the element), which lets the target's handler run first, *then* blocks the bubble to the full-card nav overlay. `Switch` was the lone outlier — the only `onClickCapture` in the entire `web/src` tree.
- **DONE (fix, one-token):** `onClickCapture` → `onClick` on the `Switch` wrapper span (bubble-phase stop, matching its siblings) + a comment explaining the phase choice. The click now reaches the switch button (fires the toggle) and is then prevented from bubbling to the nav overlay.
- **Guard (new `bento.test.tsx`):** two tests locking both halves of the card-control contract — (1) clicking the switch fires its `onToggle` AND does not reach an enclosing (nav-overlay stand-in) `onClick`; (2) a `disabled` switch is a no-op. Proven to FAIL against the pre-fix capture-phase code (`onToggle` called 0 times) and pass after — a true regression guard, not a tautology.
- **Zero visual / zero primitive-count change:** behavior-only (event phase); no raw element added/removed, no pixels moved. `bento.test.tsx` is a `.test.tsx` so the scanner excludes it — ratchet + audit JSON unchanged (`{ rawButton: 376, rawInput: 187, rawDialog: 0, inlineFontWeight: 7 }`).
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean; vitest **91/91** (+2 bento tests; ratchet green at 187); build green; visual **64/64** (no baseline moved); UI-validated on the live gateway — clicked "Send on Enter" on the Chat card: it toggled off→on and stayed on `#/settings` (no unwanted navigation), round-tripped back to its original state. Committed atomically (`fix(settings):`, DCO-signed, no agent trailers, on `feature-design-system-consistency`, not pushed).

### 2026-07-23 — cycle 34 (cluster 3: the compound-search primitive — SearchField, 12 fields) — owner-authorized scope & style
- **Context (owner decisions via AskUserQuestion):** cycle 32's "NEXT" foreshadowed this — the compound-search cluster (`type="search"` + a clear affordance) is a distinct role from the bare leading-icon `TextInput`, so it gets its OWN `ui/` primitive rather than overloading `TextInput`. Two owner rulings framed the build: (1) **clear-(X) style = "spring-pop circle"** (soft `rounded-full size-6` circle, framer-motion `AnimatePresence` scale 0→1 `bounce.subtle`, `whileTap` 0.88, `X size 14`, idle `text-on-surface-low` / hover `bg-surface-highest`); (2) **scope = "Everything (A + B)"** — migrate ALL compound-search fields, both the absolute-overlay family (Family A, 8 fields) AND the flex-row chip family (Family B, 4 palette fields), despite the AskUserQuestion preview flagging Family B as bordering on redesign.
- **Soul-honoring mitigation for the A+B scope (consistency, not redesign):** ONE primitive, TWO structural variants, because the app genuinely ships two search shells and folding one onto the other WOULD be a redesign. `overlay` (default) codifies the dominant list/page search (relative box, magnifier pinned `left-3`, input `pl-9 pr-9`, clear pinned `right-2.5`, owns `type="search"` + Escape-to-clear + the size→height/radius scale + focus ring). `inline` preserves each palette's own transparent flex-chip identity (bg/radius/padding stay on the caller's row; the input is `bg-transparent`, the magnifier + trailing slot are flex children; no height, no focus ring). Every value is a shape already in-tree — the clear-X converges on the owner's spring-pop, the geometry inherits the cycle-32-blessed `left-3`/`pl-9` inset.
- **DONE (the primitive):** `web/src/ui/SearchField.tsx` — `SearchField` with `variant` (overlay|inline), `size` (sm/md/lg → overlay h-8/9/10 @ 0.8125/0.8125/0.9375rem, rounded-md/md/pill), `surface`, built-in spring-pop `ClearButton` (`dense` shrinks it for compact inline rows), `clearable` opt-out (the ⌘K/⌘P palettes clear by Esc/backspace convention — they decline the button), `clearOnEscape` (inline opt-in; overlay always clears), `trailingSlot` (a `<kbd>esc</kbd>`/spinner after the clear-X), `inlineIconSize`, and passthroughs (`inputRef`, `onFocus`, `spellCheck`/`autoCapitalize`/`autoCorrect`, `name`). Guarded by `web/src/ui/SearchField.test.tsx` (13 tests: named type=search, ariaLabel-over-placeholder, clear-X show/click/Escape per variant, caller-onKeyDown pre-empt, clearable opt-out, trailing slot, inputRef, **+ the two validate-as-a-user regression guards below**).
- **DONE (12 field migrations — Family A overlay, 8):** `app/CommandPalette.tsx`* , `pages/settings/SettingsHome.tsx` (hero), `pages/settings/DiagnosticsPanel.tsx`, `pages/settings/LocalModelManager.tsx`, `pages/settings/ModelsPanel.tsx`, `pages/files/FilesSection.tsx`, `pages/code/WorkspacePicker.tsx`, `ui/ListControls.tsx` (in-`ui/`, ratchet-exempt). **Family B inline, 4:** `app/CommandPalette.tsx` (⌘K), `pages/chat/PromptPalette.tsx`, `pages/code/CodeCockpitPage.tsx` (⌘P quick-open), `pages/tasks/TasksListPage.tsx`. Each migration pruned only truly-dead lucide symbols (`Search`/`X` removed where the file no longer hand-rolls them; kept where still used elsewhere) — verified by a clean typecheck.
- **DEVIATION (documented) — ListControls lost its focus-within magnifier micro-animation:** the shared primitive draws a static leading magnifier (the 7-of-8 overlay majority), so ListControls' bespoke focus-within icon nudge was dropped to match the majority rather than special-case the primitive. Consistency-over-flourish, flagged for owner review.
- **Deliberate pixel move (owner-authorized) — SettingsHome hero search normalized `h-11`/`0.95rem` → lg `h-10`/`0.9375rem`:** an off-ramp size snapping onto the blessed scale (~4px vertical reflow). This is the ONE mover the top-level route harness snapshots → **Settings light+dark baselines regenerated intentionally** (`e2e/__screenshots__/visual.spec.ts/settings-{light,dark}-darwin.png`), recorded here per the "regenerate touched surfaces intentionally, never silently keep/revert" rule. All other movers live in interaction-gated palettes/pickers the route harness doesn't capture.
- **Two validate-as-a-user regressions caught in the live UI (pure code review missed both) and root-caused, not worked around:**
  1. **Inline focus ring bleed** — the inline palette input inherited `focus:ring-2 focus:ring-inset focus:ring-primary/50` from the shared `INPUT_CHROME`, drawing a Coral inset RECTANGLE inside the rounded ⌘K row (new chrome = a redesign of a hero surface). Verified against git HEAD that all 4 inline originals shipped `outline-none` with NO ring → split the ring into overlay-only `OVERLAY_FOCUS`; inline keeps `outline-none`. Confirmed live at computed-style level (inline className `outline-none text-[0.9375rem]`, boxShadow none). **Locked by a new unit assertion** ("applies the inset focus ring only in the overlay variant").
  2. **Native `::-webkit-search-cancel-button` glyph** — `type="search"` makes Chromium auto-render a native `×` once there's a value; it double-rendered beside the spring-pop clear-X and gave the `clearable={false}` palettes a clear button they declined. The originals had no `type` so never showed it. Suppressed with `[&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:hidden` on `INPUT_CHROME` (both variants). Confirmed live: the compiled rule is in the served bundle and the Settings hero shows exactly ONE clear affordance (the 24×24 spring-pop circle), no double glyph; the ⌘K palette shows none. **Locked by a new unit assertion** ("suppresses the native webkit search-cancel glyph in both variants").
- **FALSE POSITIVE (classified, unchanged, no suppression):** `CodeCockpitPage.tsx` L740/741 side-tab hook finding — a toast card `borderLeft: 3px solid ${tone}` where `tone` is a severity color. Domain-appropriate, pre-existing, out of this task's scope. Left as-is with no inline suppression (impeccable suppressions need explicit owner sign-off).

### 2026-07-23 — cycle 35 (cluster 4a: the canonical numeric stepper — NumberField, 3 verbatim dups) — byte-identical, autonomous
- **Context:** cycle 34's "NEXT" foreshadowed the numeric steppers as "a distinct role, its own primitive." Scope this cycle = the THREE near-**verbatim-duplicated** clamp-on-blur steppers, which share BOTH the exact chrome AND the identical fiddly behavior (local string state so a half-typed value isn't clobbered mid-edit; on blur/Enter → parse → clamp to `[min,max]` → commit only if changed; revert an empty/NaN entry to the last good value): `ChatPanel.NumberInput` (+ its `NumberRow` wrapper's inline twin), `AgentDefaultsPanel.NumberRow`'s inline `<input type=number>`, and `InboxSettingsPanel.NumDays`. This is a true byte-identical consolidation — it dedups LOGIC, not just chrome — so it proceeded autonomously (no taste call to surface). The other numeric inputs (ScheduleForm cadence, MemoryPanel weights) are genuinely different roles and stay out of scope.
- **DONE (the primitive):** `web/src/ui/forms.tsx` `NumberField` — `value`/`onChange`/`min`/`max`/`step`/`ariaLabel` + a single visual axis `width` (`w-24` default; `w-20` opt for the narrow Inbox field — the ONE axis the three dups varied). Chrome is invariant and reproduces the prior hand-rolled field token-for-token (`h-8 rounded-md bg-surface-high px-2 text-right text-[0.8125rem] text-on-surface tabular-nums outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-inset focus:ring-primary/50`). Deliberately NOT the standard `TextInput` size/surface scale: a stepper is a distinct role (fixed-width, right-aligned, tabular-nums) that must not be forced onto the full-width text field — same call cycle 30 made. Accessible-name resolution mirrors the family: an explicit `ariaLabel` wins; else it claims a wrapping `ui/forms` `Field` label via `aria-labelledby` (`useFieldLabelId()`). `settingsUI.Row` is a plain label div (NOT a `FieldLabelCtx` provider), so all three call sites pass `ariaLabel={label}` — a pure a11y GAIN, since the raw inputs they replace had **no accessible name at all**.
- **DONE (3 call-site migrations):** `pages/settings/ChatPanel.tsx` (deleted the local `NumberInput` component; both its call sites — the warm-pool-size `Row` and the `NumberRow` helper — now render `NumberField`), `pages/settings/AgentDefaultsPanel.tsx` (`NumberRow` renderer's inline input → `NumberField`; 5 live steppers: max-subagents/max-turns/timeout/min-memory/soft-stop), `pages/settings/InboxSettingsPanel.tsx` (`NumDays` helper inlined at the call site as `NumberField width="w-20"` + the kept "days" suffix span; dead helper deleted). Backend serialization invariant verified: `AppConfig.to_dict()` uses `asdict(self.agent)` so every `agent.*` field is always present — AgentDefaults' prior `?? ''` fallback normalizes safely to `Number(cfg[field] ?? min ?? 0)` with zero pixel/behavior change (the panel also gates on `FormSkeleton` until `cfg` loads).
- **Ratchet DOWN (live-measured via `scanDrift()`, not guessed) in the same commit:** `rawInput` **176→173** (−3: the three non-`ui/` field migrations; the primitive itself lives in `ui/`, exempt). `rawButton` (369) / `rawDialog` (0) / `inlineFontWeight` (7) unchanged.
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean; vitest **113/113** (+9 new `NumberField` tests in `forms.test.tsx`: default+width chrome byte-check, clamp-to-max on blur, no-commit-when-unchanged, empty/NaN revert, Enter-commits, external-value resync, ariaLabel name, Field `aria-labelledby` claim; ratchet green at 173); build green (SPA served live via the `static/dist` symlink, bundle `index-DkYJj9np.js`); visual **64/64 — NO baseline moved** (widths preserved exactly → byte-identical; the Settings route shell is pixel-unchanged). UI-validated all three panels live on `127.0.0.1:10400` (empty-seed E2E instance, verified served bundle hash): all 9 steppers render at the exact prior geometry (`w-24`=96px / `w-20`=80px, `h-8`=32px) with correct accessible names + suffixes; clamp-on-blur confirmed end-to-end through the real backend PATCH round-trip (Agent max-subagents 99→16, Inbox retention 0→1, Chat autocompact 5000→90); the conditional warm-pool-TTL row correctly appears when pool size >0; all dev state restored. Committed atomically (`refactor(design):`, DCO-signed, no agent trailers, on `feature-design-system-consistency`, **not pushed**).

### 2026-07-23 — cycle 36 (cluster 4b: T2.1 type-scale normalization — app-wide off-ramp `text-[…]` sweep + ramp reconciliation) — pixel-moving, owner-directed
- **Context:** cycle 35's "NEXT" flagged the off-ramp text literals (MemoryPanel:272 `text-[0.8rem]` and its kin) as a distinct **T2.1 text-scale normalization** — explicitly *not* to be smuggled into a chrome migration. Unlike every prior byte-identical chrome cycle, normalizing a font-size literal MOVES rendered pixels, so this is a taste/pixel call: surfaced to the owner before editing. **Four owner decisions** (3 AskUserQuestion rounds): (1) **scope = full uniform sweep** — every off-ramp `text-[Nrem]`/`text-[Npx]` literal app-wide, not just the one flagged site; (2) **sub-floor text = add a blessed `caption` tier** (0.75rem/12px) rather than round the ~sub-0.8125rem drift up; (3) **`0.875rem` (166×) snaps DOWN** to `small` 0.8125rem; (4) **`1rem`/16px snaps UP** to the existing `body-l` 1.0625rem rung (no new "subtitle" tier — a mid-round correction: I had proposed one on the false premise that 16–17px "wasn't on the machine ramp," then found `tokens.css` already defines `body-l`/`label-l` at 1.0625rem, surfaced the correction, and the owner chose the existing rung), **and reconcile the DESIGN.md frontmatter to mirror the true tokens.css ramp**.
- **DONE (ramp reconciliation — `web/DESIGN.md` + `web/src/design/tokens.css`):** the impeccable font-size detector parses DESIGN.md's YAML `typography` frontmatter as the blessed scale (±0.5px); that block was an incomplete *subset* of the richer `tokens.css` `data-type` role system and had been false-flagging legit shipped rungs (1.0625/1.75/2/2.25rem). Replaced the frontmatter `typography` block with a **strict superset** mirroring tokens.css exactly (display-l/m/s · headline-l/m · title-l · body-l · body-m/label-m · body-s · **caption** · mono) — verified a superset: it *adds* 0.75/1.0625/1.75/2/2.25rem and *removes nothing* the old block blessed, so it cannot introduce a new finding. Added the new `[data-type="caption"]` role to tokens.css (`font-size: 0.75rem; line-height: 1rem; font-variation-settings: "wght" 470` — mid-weight like label-s, since the sub-floor text is overwhelmingly chip/badge/timestamp meta). Updated the prose `### Hierarchy` with the discrete-rungs sentence (`0.75 · 0.8125 · 0.9375 · 1.0625 · 1.25 · 1.5 · 1.75 · 2 · 2.25 · 2.625rem`) and a Caption bullet. **Blessed px set after reconciliation: 12·13·15·17·20·24·28·32·36·42px.**
- **DONE (the sweep — 139 source files):** every off-ramp `text-[Nrem]`/`text-[Npx]` class literal snapped to the nearest blessed rung via a deterministic, idempotent throwaway transform (auditable, then deleted). **~934 literals snapped:** **664 → caption 0.75rem** (from 0.6/0.62/0.625/0.65/0.66/0.68/0.6875/0.7/0.72/0.74/0.76/0.78rem), **243 → small 0.8125rem** (from 0.8/0.82/0.84/0.85/0.875rem), **11 → body 0.9375rem** (0.9/0.95rem), **16 → body-l 1.0625rem** (1/1.05/1.15rem/16px). Two paired chat edits carry the 1rem→1.0625rem intent through inline styles too: `ChatPage.tsx:2441` composer `text-[16px]`→`text-[1.0625rem]` (still ≥16px, preserves mobile-no-zoom) and `MessageUser.tsx:33` user-bubble `fontSize: '16px'`→`'1.0625rem'`. The 0.75rem tier needed only *legalization* (no code change) at its ~295 pre-existing sites once the caption role was blessed. Diff is surgical: **0 non-`text-[…]` changed lines** across all 139 files besides those 2 intentional inline edits + the DESIGN.md/tokens.css comment additions (verified line-by-line).
- **Deliberately OUT of scope (documented, not swept):** the pre-existing off-ramp sizes in **rendered-document / non-chrome** contexts — `tokens.css` `.doc` scoped-prose block (h1 1.9rem, h2 1.45rem, h3 1.2rem, h4 1.02rem, base 1rem: an article/markdown typography scale distinct from app chrome), `content/exporters.ts` (1.2rem, export HTML), `ui/widget/widgetSrcdoc.ts` (14px, sandboxed widget iframe), `ui/composer/liveMarkdown.ts` (CodeMirror editor-theme mono), and `fonts.css` brand `@font-face` families. These pre-date this cycle (proven: the OLD frontmatter didn't bless them either), and folding a rendered-document type scale into a chrome-consistency sweep would be a *document-rendering redesign* — a separate taste call, not a normalization. Left for a future dedicated pass.
- **No ratchet move:** text-size has NO in-repo CI ratchet (`consistencyAudit.report.ts` tracks chrome/weights/color, not font-size); the real gate for this sweep = the impeccable font-size detector staying clean on changed files + the Playwright visual baselines. `docs/design/consistency-audit.json` diff was **timestamp-only** (`generatedAt`, zero real signal — a font-size sweep touches no primitive/a11y counts) → reverted, kept out of the commit. Baseline unchanged: `{ rawButton: 369, rawInput: 173, rawDialog: 0, inlineFontWeight: 7 }`.
- **Evidence:** impeccable font-size detector (`detect.mjs --scope type`) **clean (EXIT=0) across all 139 changed files** — control-verified live (an off-ramp `0.66rem` probe flags; a `0.75rem` probe passes, confirming the reconciled caption rung loads); typecheck clean; vitest **113/113** (incl. the `forms.test.tsx:57` negative guard `not.toContain('text-[0.875rem]')`, correctly the ONLY surviving `0.875rem` — a test-assertion string, excluded from the sweep); build green (`index-DFNQ27Vp.js` + `index-D29aUe84.js`); visual **64/64**. **2 baselines regenerated** — `tools-light-darwin.png` + `tools-dark-darwin.png` (~3% pixels; ToolsPage is the one route shell whose top-level text moved: 0.7→0.75, 0.875→0.8125, 1rem→1.0625 title) — diff confirmed purely intended text-size reflow before `--update-snapshots`. Most swept text lives in un-snapshotted settings sub-panels / chat / memory studio, so no other baseline moved. UI-validated live on `127.0.0.1:10400` (empty-seed E2E instance, verified served bundle hash): Tools / Settings·agent / Memory studio all render clean, and DOM font-size introspection confirmed every rendered leaf is on-ramp (192@12px, 79@13px, 16@15px; the lone raw 16px was the non-rendered `<title>` in `<head>`). Committed atomically (`refactor(design): T2.1 normalize off-ramp text sizes to the blessed ramp`, DCO-signed, no agent trailers, on `feature-design-system-consistency`, **not pushed**).
- **NEXT:** the type ramp is now uniformly enforced across app chrome + the frontmatter mirrors tokens.css, so any NEW off-ramp `text-[…]` literal reddens the impeccable hook. Remaining T2 threads: the **rendered-document `.doc` type scale** (the deliberately-deferred taste call above — decide whether it earns its own blessed document-tier set or should converge onto the chrome ramp) and any non-`text-[…]` inline `fontSize` literals in chrome (chat pair handled this cycle; audit the rest). Chrome-migration clusters (raw-button consolidation, `rawButton: 369`) remain the larger S2 body of work.

### 2026-07-23 — cycle 37 (T2.3: extract the `SquareIconButton` dense-square icon primitive + migrate its five hand-rolled copies) — new shared primitive, owner-directed
- **Context:** cycle 36's "NEXT" flagged raw-button consolidation (`rawButton: 369`) as the larger S2 body. Before touching call sites I surfaced two taste calls (2 AskUserQuestion rounds): (1) **Raw-btn scope = "authorize as intended fixes"** — proceed cluster-by-cluster, update Playwright baselines where motion/pixels legitimately move, owner reviews snapshots after each cycle (enables the full 369 consolidation over time); (2) **square icon-btn on-state = "bg-fill hover + press motion"** — idle `text-on-surface-low` / hover `bg-surface-high + text-on-surface` / `on` primary tint / `disabled` opacity-40 / motion `whileTap` press-spring, reduced-motion safe ("matches ContentSurface, FileViewer; aligns with the animated IconButton doctrine"). The dense **square** icon-button (size-7, rounded-md, small glyph) is a **genuine missing primitive**, distinct from the round/large-glyph/pill `ui/IconButton` — it was hand-rolled as five separate `IconBtn` copies. This cycle extracts it (T2.3) and migrates the family. Soul guardrail honoured: consolidation onto the shape that already shipped five times, not a redesign.
- **DONE (the primitive — `web/src/ui/SquareIconButton.tsx` + `SquareIconButton.test.tsx`):** new shared primitive under `ui/` (exempt from the scanner). Renders `grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-md transition-colors` — idle `text-on-surface-low`, hover `bg-surface-high + text-on-surface`, `on` (selected) coral tint (`text-primary` + a token-routed `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) 14%, transparent)` bg chip), `disabled` `opacity-40 cursor-not-allowed` with onClick suppressed. Press springs via framer `whileTap` (expressiveness-scaled through `expr()`, `useReducedMotion()`-safe, `spring.spatialFast`). Accepts either an `icon` (LucideIcon, `iconSize` default 14) or `children` (for state-swapping glyphs — spinner⇄wifi, X⇄Pencil), a required `label` (accessible name **and** `title` tooltip). **Naming collision resolved:** the copies overloaded `active` to mean *busy→disabled* (settings: `disabled={active}`) in some, *selected→coral* (ContentSurface: `color: active ? primary`) in others — the primitive splits this into orthogonal **`on`** (selected) + **`disabled`** (busy). 6 tests pin the four visual states + the semantics (idle chrome / on-tint / disabled dim+inert+aria / disabled-never-lit / label-as-name-and-title + click-fires-once / children render).
- **DONE (the 5-copy consolidation):** two byte-identical settings copies — `pages/settings/MultiInstanceCard.tsx` (Test `active={testing}`→`disabled={testing}`, Edit `on={editing}`, Delete; local `IconBtn` def deleted → file's raw-button 1→0) and `pages/settings/ModelBackends.tsx` (Test `active`→`disabled`, View-models `on={showModels}`, Edit `on={editing}`, Delete; local `IconBtn` deleted → 2→1, the surviving count being the **password-toggle `<button>` deliberately left for a later slice** — a distinct role positioned *inside* the input via `inputCls`) — plus three `ui/` near-variants: `ui/content/ContentSurface.tsx` (word-wrap `active={wrap}`→`on={wrap} iconSize={13}`, Copy, Export; local `IconBtn` deleted, `motion`/`spring` retained by `ToggleBtn`'s `layoutId` pill), `ui/widget/WidgetFrame.tsx` (Save-as-artifact `disabled={savePending} on={saved}`, Download, Open-in-new-tab, Minimize/Expand; local `IconBtn` with color-only on-state deleted), `ui/widget/ReactWidgetFrame.tsx` (Open-in-new-tab, Minimize/Expand; simplest local `IconBtn` deleted). The three `ui/` copies' **color-only on-state** (`style={{ color: primary }}`) homogenized onto the primitive's bg-chip on-state — an authorized pixel move (owner decision 2 above), consistent with the settings/ContentSurface tint.
- **Ratchet: `rawButton` 369 → 367** (delta **−2**, in the same commit — `web/src/design/primitiveAdoption.baseline.json`). The delta is 2 not ~5 because the scanner (`consistencyAudit.report.ts`) counts `<button>` **text occurrences**, and everything under `web/src/ui/` is exempt: so the three `ui/` `IconBtn` defs never counted, and each settings copy's single `IconBtn` **function definition** counted exactly once → MultiInstanceCard(−1) + ModelBackends(−1). Verified live via a throwaway `scanDrift()` probe before/after. `docs/design/consistency-audit.json` regenerated by the vitest run carries **real signal** (ModelBackends raw-button 2→1, MultiInstanceCard 1→0, filesScanned +1 for the new primitive file, WidgetFrame drift snippet line +1 from the added import) — a legitimate ride-along, kept in the commit (not timestamp-only, unlike cycle 36's font sweep).
- **Doc:** `docs/design/patterns.md` gains a `### SquareIconButton` entry under Buttons — canonical shape, props table, the `on`-vs-`disabled` naming note, usage snippet, and "migrate to it when you see a hand-rolled `size-7 … rounded-md … place-items-center` icon `<button>`; the round pill (`IconButton`) and this square dense form are the two canonical icon-action shapes — pick by density, don't hand-roll a third."
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean (one self-corrected miss: migrated ContentSurface's call sites + deleted its local def but initially forgot the import → typecheck flagged it → added `import { SquareIconButton } from '../SquareIconButton'` in the same pass); vitest **119/119** (incl. `primitiveAdoption.test.ts` against 367 + the 6 new SquareIconButton tests); build green (chunk-size warnings pre-existing); visual **64/64 with ZERO baseline changes** — every migrated surface lives in un-snapshotted settings sub-panels or interaction-gated content/widget toolbars, so no route-shell snapshot legitimately moved (owner-authorized baseline updates were therefore unneeded this cycle). UI-validated live on `127.0.0.1:10400` (empty-seed E2E instance): created a scratch `.md` in the dev Files workspace, entered Edit mode to surface the ContentSurface toolbar, toggled word-wrap ON, and confirmed via `browser_evaluate` the on-state renders exactly as designed — `className` includes `text-primary`, computed `color: rgb(255,107,91)` (=#ff6b5b brand coral), `backgroundColor: color(srgb 1 0.419608 0.356863 / 0.14)`, inline `background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) 14%, transparent)` (token-routed, no hardcoded hex), box 28×28, borderRadius 12px; scratch file then deleted via the confirmDelete dialog (workspace restored). Committed atomically (`refactor(design): T2.3 extract SquareIconButton dense icon primitive`, DCO-signed, no agent trailers, on `feature-design-system-consistency`, **not pushed**).
- **NEXT:** the dense-square role now has a canonical home + ratchet floor at 367. Authorized follow-on raw-button clusters (owner decision 1, "intended fixes"): the retained **ModelBackends password-toggle** (input-embedded, its own slice), **FileViewer** icon buttons, **ToolsPage** (~8 rawButton incl. the danger-hover Remove), and the always-primary Plus buttons in **AgentDefaultsPanel/InboxSettingsPanel**. Each is a distinct role/motion decision — surface pixel-moving ones (esp. any that move a route-shell Playwright baseline) before editing.

### 2026-07-23 — cycle 38 (T2.3 cont.: grow `SquareIconButton` with `tone="danger"` + `title`, migrate families A/B/C across 9 files) — owner-directed adoption sweep
- **Context:** cycle 37's "NEXT" enumerated the authorized follow-on clusters. Before editing I mapped the remaining `size-7 rounded-md` icon-button hand-rolls into three role families and surfaced scope + the one new visual as **2 AskUserQuestion rounds**: **Q1 (scope) = "A + B + C"** — migrate all three: **A** = clean bg-fill adopters already matching the primitive byte-for-byte (TerminalDrawer, FileViewer), **B** = color-only-hover sites that GAIN the bg-fill hover (an authorized pixel move, same as cycle 37's `ui/` copies), **C** = danger-hover deletes needing a new destructive variant; **Q2 (danger hover) = "Glyph-tint only"** — hover tints the glyph red (`hover:text-danger`) with **no** background fill, which is byte-identical to what the existing delete buttons already render (they were `text-on-surface-low hover:text-danger` with no chip). Soul guardrail honoured: this is adoption of the shape that already shipped, plus a variant that codifies the delete-hover the copies already used — not a redesign.
- **DONE (the primitive grows — `web/src/ui/SquareIconButton.tsx` + test):** added two orthogonal props. **`tone: 'neutral' | 'danger'`** (default `neutral`): `danger` swaps the neutral hover (`hover:bg-surface-high hover:text-on-surface`) for `hover:text-danger` (glyph-tint, no fill); ignored while `on` (a selected danger control is not a state that exists in the migrated set, and the coral `on`-tint wins by precedence in the className ternary). **`title?: string`** (defaults to `label`): a tooltip override for the sites whose hover hint legitimately differs from the accessible name (e.g. LocalModelManager's gated-download reason, ToolsPage's "Reconnect this server"). A 7th test pins the danger tone: asserts `text-on-surface-low` + `hover:text-danger`, and `not.toContain('hover:bg-surface-high')` / `not.toContain('text-primary')`.
- **DONE (9 call-site migrations, families A/B/C):** **A (byte-identical bg-fill adopters):** `pages/terminal/TerminalDrawer.tsx` (New-session spinner⇄Plus, Open-full, Hide-terminal; 5 raw→2), `pages/files/browse/FileViewer.tsx` (compact Save-as-artifact + Reveal-in-Finder; the non-compact text "Artifact" button and the `<a download>` anchor **deliberately stayed raw** — an anchor can't be a `<button>`, the text button has a visible label; 4→2). **B (color-only-hover → bg-fill, authorized pixel move):** `pages/settings/ProviderCard.tsx` (Recheck spinner + Configure toggle preserving its rotating chevron; 6→4), `pages/settings/ProviderConfigForm.tsx` + `pages/settings/ModelBackends.tsx` (password show/hide eye toggles — **wrapped in a positioning `<span>`** so `whileTap`'s transform doesn't clobber `-translate-y-1/2`; 1→0 each, retiring cycle 37's deliberately-kept ModelBackends toggle). **C (danger deletes → `tone="danger"`):** `pages/tools/ToolsPage.tsx` (Reconnect + danger Remove-server; 8→6), `pages/apps/AppsSection.tsx` (two danger Remove-source + a copy-command Copy⇄Check button; 8→5), `pages/settings/MemoryPanel.tsx` (StudioInspector danger Delete; 9→8), `pages/settings/LocalModelManager.tsx` (Cancel + the dual Download/Delete button whose `title` carries the gated-license reason; 2→0 — the site that motivated the `title` prop).

Verified exact via a throwaway `scanDrift()` probe before/after: every touched file's per-file decrement matches (TerminalDrawer −3, FileViewer −2, ProviderCard −2, ProviderConfigForm −1, ModelBackends −1, ToolsPage −2, AppsSection −3, MemoryPanel −1, LocalModelManager −2 → −17). `docs/design/consistency-audit.json` regenerated by vitest carries **real signal** (per-file raw-button decrements for all 9 files; LocalModelManager 2→0 and ProviderConfigForm 1→0 drop off the list entirely; score-rank reshuffle) — a legitimate ride-along, kept in the commit.

Committed atomically (`refactor(design): T2.2 extract duplicated GraphZoomControls overlay`, DCO-signed, no agent trailers, on `feature-design-system-consistency`, **not pushed**).

### 2026-07-24 — cycle 41 (T2.2: consolidate the duplicated `VariableRow` prompt-variable editor — owner-authorized, "use your judgement") — canonical-copy decision made
- **Context:** the cycle-40 exhaustion sweep found `VariableRow` (the typed prompt-variable row: name/type/required/description/default/+choices) **verbatim-triplicated** across `pages/prompts/PromptForm.tsx`, `pages/prompts/PromptEditFields.tsx`, and `pages/prompts/SnippetForm.tsx`, and flagged it as the **one duplicate-component dedup that was NOT byte-identical** — the three copies diverge in two ways, so it needed a canonical-version + copy decision (queued for owner as item 3 of 4). The owner answered **"use your judgement for 1, 2, 3 and 4 all"** — authorizing autonomous resolution. This cycle executes item 3. Soul guardrail honoured: dedup a duplicated component onto its richest existing copy, no new visual language.
- **The two divergences + decisions:** (1) **a11y/autofill richness** — `PromptForm`'s copy carries `useId()`-prefixed `name=` attrs (`var-name-`/`var-type-`/`var-desc-`/`var-default-`/`var-opts-`, browser-autofill defeat) + `aria-label`s + a `relative` select wrapper; the other two copies lacked the `name=` attrs. **Decision: canonical = PromptForm's copy** (strictly more accessible; the other two GAIN the a11y attrs — a pure gain, visually byte-identical). (2) **description placeholder copy** — snippets say `placeholder="Description"` (a snippet is *inserted*), prompts say `"Description (shown when invoked)"` (a prompt is *invoked*). **Decision: make it a `descriptionPlaceholder` prop** defaulting to the prompt wording; `SnippetForm` passes `"Description"` — the domain copy difference is preserved exactly, not flattened.
- **DONE (the shared component — `web/src/pages/prompts/VariableRow.tsx` + `.test.tsx`):** extracted verbatim from PromptForm's copy. Signature `{ v, onChange, onRemove, descriptionPlaceholder='Description (shown when invoked)' }`. **Kept in `pages/prompts/` (NOT `ui/`)** on purpose: it imports `PromptVariable`/`PromptVarType` + `VAR_TYPES` from the prompt domain (`./promptMeta`, `../../lib/api`), so a `ui/`→`pages/` import would invert the dependency boundary (the cycle-29 rule). Being under `pages/` it stays scanner-counted, so the dedup shrinks the ratchet (unlike the `ui/`-exempt component dedups). 4 tests pin: a11y names + `var-*-` autofill attrs sharing the `useId` suffix; the description-placeholder default + snippet override (via rerender); select-type reveals the choices field; name-sanitize (`[^a-zA-Z0-9_]`→`_` via `fireEvent.change` — React's controlled-input value tracker ignores a raw native event, so `fireEvent.change` is required to fire `onChange`) + required-toggle + remove wiring.
- **DONE (3 call-site migrations):** all three files import `VariableRow` and delete their local def; unused `useId`/`X`/`VAR_TYPES` lucide/type imports dropped (each verified used ONLY in the removed def — `PromptEditFields` self-corrected: I first over-dropped `PromptVariable`, still used at its `updateVar`, and restored it before typecheck). `PromptForm` + `PromptEditFields` call sites unchanged (default placeholder = prompt wording, matching their prior literal); `SnippetForm` call site adds `descriptionPlaceholder="Description"` to preserve its distinct copy.
- **Ratchet: `rawButton` 344 → 340 (−4), `rawInput` 173 → 163 (−10)**, same commit. The scanner counts raw `<button>`/`<input>` by **text occurrence**, so removing 2 duplicate copies of a row with 2 raw buttons (required-toggle + X) and 5 raw inputs (name/desc/default/choices + … ) each = −4 button / −10 input. Verified exact via a throwaway `scanDrift()` probe before/after (created, confirmed, deleted). `docs/design/consistency-audit.json` regenerated by vitest carries real per-file signal (the three prompt files' raw-button/input decrements) — legitimate ride-along, kept in commit.
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean (after the self-corrected `PromptVariable` restore); vitest **126/126** (incl. `primitiveAdoption.test.ts` against 340/163 + the 4 new VariableRow tests; one self-fixed test-authoring bug — the sanitize test first used `input.value=`+raw native event, which React's value tracker ignores → switched to `fireEvent.change`, a test bug not a product regression); build green; visual **64/64 with ZERO baseline movement**. **Validated as a user live on `127.0.0.1:10400`** (after `make web-build` refreshed the served bundle — the browser had cached a pre-rebuild `index.html`; forced a root reload): **Prompt editor** (`/prompts/new?kind=user`) — Add-variable mounts the migrated row with all 5 type options, required-toggle flips optional→required, select-type reveals the "Variable choices" field, `browser_evaluate` confirmed every field has a distinct `var-*-` `name=` sharing the `useId` suffix (`:rt:`) and description placeholder = **`Description (shown when invoked)`**; **Snippet editor** (`/prompts/new?kind=snippets`) — same shared row, description placeholder = **`Description`** (the prop override). Both editors render the single shared component exactly as designed. Committed atomically (`refactor(design): T2.2 consolidate the duplicated VariableRow prompt-variable editor`, DCO-signed, no agent trailers, on `feature-design-system-consistency`, **not pushed**).
- **NEXT:** the duplicate-component lane is now fully drained (GraphZoomControls in c39 + VariableRow here were the two real dedup targets from the c40 sweep; `Centered` declined as over-engineering). Remaining owner-authorized items from "use your judgement 1–4": **item 4** — genuinely-repeated utility className strings → possible micro-primitives (SectionLabel/Chip/FormFooter), extract only verbatim-repeated strings, skip anything needing invented API; **item 2** — the always-primary add-item Plus buttons (AgentDefaultsPanel/InboxSettingsPanel), an always-primary variant or `on`-chip pixel move; **item 1** — the mixed-role mega-pages (CodeCockpit ~23, ProjectsSection ~20, ChatPage ~19, LoopPlanReview ~13, CodePlanReview ~12, LoopCockpitPage ~11, WorkflowForm ~10), one page/cycle with per-role triage (primary→Button, round-large-glyph→IconButton, dense-square→SquareIconButton), regenerating baselines where pixels legitimately move.
