### 2026-07-24 — cycle 43 (T2.3: migrate the two always-coral add-item Plus buttons to `SquareIconButton` — owner-authorized item 2, "use your judgement") — outlier brought to the existing dense-square idiom
- **Context:** item 2 of the owner's "use your judgement 1–4." Cycle 40/42 had flagged these as needing "an always-primary variant or an `on`-chip pixel move" — i.e. a taste call. Re-examining them under the soul guardrail (*consistency, not redesign — bring outliers to the existing system*) reframed it: the two buttons in `pages/settings/{AgentDefaultsPanel,InboxSettingsPanel}.tsx` are **byte-identical to each other** (`grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-md text-primary` + `<Plus size={15}/>`, conditionally rendered when the sibling input is non-empty, **no `aria-label`, no hover/press state**) and are **outliers on two axes at once**: (a) their own sibling remove-**X** buttons in the same pill rows are neutral ink-low, and (b) the app's canonical "type-a-value → click **+** to append beside the input" idiom (`pages/tasks/formControls.tsx` ×2, `pages/loops/LoopPlanReview.tsx`) idles **neutral**, not coral. So this isn't "invent an always-coral variant" — it's "stop being the one always-coral feedback-less + in an app whose adds are neutral."
- **Decision (surfaced for owner veto, not blocked on):** migrate both to the existing **`SquareIconButton` neutral tone** (`ui/SquareIconButton.tsx`), the primitive built for exactly this dense `size-7 rounded-md` hit area ("tight action clusters… where the 40px round pill is too large"). Its base geometry classes (`grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-md`) are **byte-identical** to the bespoke button, and the `Plus` glyph stays at `iconSize={15}`. **One deliberate pixel change:** idle `text-primary` (coral) → `text-on-surface-low` (ink-low), and the button **gains** the standard hover-fill (`hover:bg-surface-high hover:text-on-surface`) + press-spring + an accessible `label` (was missing entirely — strictly an a11y improvement). This is a taste call I resolved under the owner's judgement grant; the coral→ink-low choice is flagged in the session-end report for veto. Considered and rejected: an always-coral `SquareIconButton` variant (would *codify* the outlier into the primitive — the opposite of bringing it to the system) and an `on`-tinted chip (semantically wrong: `on` means selected/toggled, not "add").
- **DONE (2 call-site migrations):** both files import `SquareIconButton` from `../../ui/SquareIconButton` and replace the inline `<button className="grid size-7 …text-primary">` with `<SquareIconButton icon={Plus} iconSize={15} label={…} onClick={…} />`. AgentDefaultsPanel's label is dynamic (`` `Add ${label.toLowerCase()}` `` — the field's own label, e.g. "Add allowed tools"); InboxSettingsPanel's is `"Add keyword"`. `Plus` import retained (still passed as `icon={Plus}`); the conditional-render guard (`adding.trim() &&` / `kw.trim() &&`) is unchanged, so the button still appears only when the input has text.
- **Ratchet: `rawButton` 340 → 338 (−2), same commit.** The scanner counts by text occurrence; removing the one raw `<button>` from each file = −2 total (each file's remaining `raw-button: 1` is the neutral remove-**X**, untouched). Verified exact via a throwaway `scanDrift()` probe (live 340 at cycle-42 tip; `primitiveAdoption.test.ts` green against the new 338 confirms exactly two removed). `rawInput`/`rawDialog`/`inlineFontWeight` untouched. `docs/design/consistency-audit.json` regenerated by vitest carries the two real per-file decrements (`AgentDefaultsPanel`/`InboxSettingsPanel` raw-button 2→1) + timestamp — legitimate signal, kept in commit.
- **Evidence:** typecheck clean (both imports resolve, `Plus` still used); vitest **129/129** (`primitiveAdoption.test.ts` against 338/163 + all prior tests unchanged); build green (`index-DrdWA18P.js`, chunk-size warning pre-existing); visual **64/64 with ZERO baseline movement** — both buttons are interaction-gated (render only when their input is non-empty), so they never appear in a route-shell snapshot; correctness rests on the live user validation below + the primitive's own `SquareIconButton.test.tsx`. **Validated as a user live on `127.0.0.1:10400`** (empty-seed E2E instance; forced a fresh root load to dodge the stale-chunk gotcha; confirmed served entry `index-DrdWA18P.js` = disk): on `#/settings/inbox`, typed into "Add keyword…" to trigger the conditional render, and `browser_evaluate` on the resulting button confirmed the real render — `aria-label`/`title` = **"Add keyword"** (the previously-missing a11y name), className = **`grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-md transition-colors text-on-surface-low hover:bg-surface-high hover:text-on-surface`** (geometry byte-identical, idle neutral, hover states present), glyph = `Plus`@15; clearing the input hid the button again (guard intact) and **no keyword persisted** (`persistedKeywords: []` — empty seed untouched, nothing to clean up). AgentDefaultsPanel is the identical primitive call (same geometry, dynamic label) so is covered by the same proof. ### 2026-07-24 — cycle 44 (S2: extract the `AddItemButton` quiet-add primitive across 5 sites — item 1 approached WorkflowForm-first, then pivoted to the byte-identical cross-file idiom it belongs to)
- **Context:** cycle 44 opened on **item 1** intending WorkflowForm (smallest mega-page, 10 raw `<button>`) as the triage warm-up. Reading it in full surfaced a scope discovery that reshaped the cycle: WorkflowForm has **zero page-local byte-identical migrations**. Its 10 buttons split into (a) **6 pixel-moving reorder glyphs** — two byte-identical ↑/↓/`X` clusters (`text-on-surface-low hover:text-on-surface disabled:opacity-30 px-1` + a `hover:text-danger` X) at the ref-step and inline-step rows; migrating these to `SquareIconButton`/`IconButton` legitimately *moves pixels* (adds a fixed square hit-area + hover chip), so they belong in a dedicated baseline-moving cycle; (b) **1 semantic list-option row** (the WorkflowRefPicker candidate button) — correctly stays raw, exactly as its twin `DependencyEditor` candidate row does (a full-width selectable option, not chrome); (c) **1 ghost `Cancel`** — instance of a 2-file idiom (`+ formControls.tsx` "Done"); and (d) **2 `Add step`/`Add a workflow` buttons** — instances of a **byte-identical cross-file idiom**.
- **Discovery → decision (pivot, surfaced not blocked):** that add-button idiom (`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-surface-container px-m h-9 text-on-surface-var text-[0.8125rem] hover:bg-surface-high transition-colors`) is duplicated **byte-for-byte across 5 static `<button>` sites in 4 files** — `WorkflowForm.tsx` ×2 (Add step / Add a workflow), `PromptEditFields.tsx`, `PromptForm.tsx`, `SnippetForm.tsx` (all "Add variable"). Migrating it *page-locally in WorkflowForm alone* would **fragment a shared idiom into two implementations** — the exact inconsistency this plan removes. So the correct unit is **the idiom across all its sites**, not the page: extract one `ui/AddItemButton` primitive (the **item-4 utility-primitive pattern**, precedent FormFooter cy42 / VariableRow cy41) and adopt it everywhere. This is a **provably byte-identical, zero-new-visual-language** consolidation — autonomous-safe, no taste call — so I proceeded. The pixel-moving WorkflowForm reorder clusters + the ghost-Cancel idiom are deliberately **left for their own cycles** so the (zero) baseline movement here stays cleanly attributable. (`formControls.tsx`'s add-prerequisite button is the same visual but a `motion.button` with bud-in/out animation + `self-start`; the scanner counts only literal `<button>`, and folding an animated variant into this primitive would either widen its API or drop the animation — a real change, so it's **not** in this byte-identical batch; noted for a follow-up if the animation can be preserved without API creep.)
- **DONE (1 primitive + 1 test + 5 call-site migrations):** new `ui/AddItemButton.tsx` — a `<button type="button">` holding the exact idiom classes via `cx(...)`, `children` = caller's leading glyph + label, optional `className` (three of five sites carry `self-start`, merged through `cx`). New `ui/AddItemButton.test.tsx` (3 tests, mirrors `FormFooter.test.tsx`): locks the 11 chrome classes, renders children, proves `self-start` rides `className` without dropping base chrome. ### 2026-07-24 — cycle 45 (item 1: migrate WorkflowForm's two reorder clusters — ↑/↓/X glyphs — to `SquareIconButton`) — the outlier text-arrow reorder brought to the dense-square idiom
- **Context:** first genuinely pixel-moving cycle of item 1, per cycle-44's NEXT. WorkflowForm's `StepEditor` renders **two byte-identical reorder clusters** — one at the ref-step row (line ~88), one at the inline-step row (line ~106) — each three raw `<button>`s (move-up ↑, move-down ↓, remove X). These are an **outlier on the reorder idiom**: WorkflowForm used bare text glyphs (`↑`/`↓`) with **no `aria-label`** and hand-rolled `disabled:opacity-30 px-1` chrome, whereas the app's other two reorder-move sites — `CodePlanReview.tsx` and `LoopPlanReview.tsx` — already use lucide `ChevronUp`/`ChevronDown` with accessible names. WorkflowForm is the divergent one; the convergent path is to bring every reorder cluster onto the same dense-square primitive over successive cycles (one page/cycle) so they all land on `SquareIconButton` without fragmenting.
- **Decision (per-role triage, dense-square rule):** the reorder glyphs are the canonical **dense square** case (small glyph, tight cluster, `size-7 rounded-md`) → `SquareIconButton` (`ui/SquareIconButton.tsx`). Move-up/down = **neutral tone** (idle `text-on-surface-low`, hover chip `hover:bg-surface-high`); remove = **`tone="danger"`** (idle neutral, `hover:text-danger`, no fill — matching the primitive's danger semantics). Glyphs swap the bare `↑`/`↓` text for lucide `ChevronUp`/`ChevronDown` (import added) and reuse the existing `X`; each gains a real `label` ("Move step up" / "Move step down" / "Remove step") — the ↑/↓ buttons previously had **no accessible name at all**, so this is also a strict a11y improvement. `disabled` wires to the existing `i === 0` / `i === steps.length - 1` edge guards. **This legitimately moves pixels** (fixed 28px square hit-area + hover chip replacing the bare-glyph `px-1`), so the cycle is baseline-moving by design — but see Evidence: the clusters are interaction+hover-gated and never enter a route-shell snapshot, so no Playwright baseline actually moved.

The wrapping `opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100` reveal container is unchanged. `X` import retained (now passed as `icon`). **Two raw `<button>` deliberately left in WorkflowForm** (audit `raw-button: 2`): the WorkflowRefPicker candidate list-row (a semantic selectable option — stays raw, exactly like DependencyEditor's candidate row) and the ghost `Cancel` (part of the 2-file ghost-`Cancel`/`Done` idiom with `formControls`, its own future cycle).

Held to the FormFooter/AddItemButton/QuietButton three-test discipline — (a) a single VERBATIM chrome string repeats 2×, (b) minimal API with **no invented visual language** (`icon`+`label`+content-relative `x`/`y`+`onPress`, forwarding a `ref` so each page keeps excluding pill-clicks from its own mouseup/mousedown selection handler, and preserving the `preventDefault`+`stopPropagation`-before-action ordering that keeps the browser selection alive), (c) **zero pixel movement** (className emitted verbatim) — **passes all three → autonomous-safe, no taste call**, so I proceeded. The differing halves stay in each page: the selection-detection `useEffect` (ChatPage's transcript-scoped vs CommentLayer's composer-aware variant) and the positioning state are **not** hoisted — extracting them would be a behavioral rewrite, forbidden by the soul guardrail. - **Context:** the cy50 "batch of taste/pixel decisions" was surfaced to the owner as a set of faithful comparison screenshots (real compiled-CSS harness, dark+light, every divergent variant beside its shared-primitive reference, labelled file:line). Execute one atomic convergence per cycle, chaining without stopping (owner's autonomous-loop-continuity directive), each with its own live validation + intentional baseline regen where pixels move.
- **RULINGS (locked):**
  - **G1 — row-action / remove-X family** → converge outliers onto **`SquareIconButton tone="danger"`** (idle ink-low, hover tints red, no fill; the restrained treatment). **Also FIX the B′ token bug:** `OllamaModelManager:88` uses `hover:text-error` → change to `hover:text-danger` (on-token; same value today via `--color-error:var(--color-danger)` but off-token). This is the `RowActionButton` family resolution: the variants (hover-reveal danger / always-visible danger / always-neutral / toggle) map to `SquareIconButton`'s existing `tone` + `on` + caller-supplied `opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover` reveal class — NO new API invented; the primitive already covers them.
  - **G2 — tonal / primary-fill convergence** → the ~44 genuinely-tonal sites converge onto **`Button variant="tonal"`** (`bg-primary/15 text-primary hover:bg-primary/25`) where geometry matches; **the `/90` near-solid `New` split-button (`ProjectsSection:514`) is PROMOTED to solid `Button variant="primary"`** (owner: "it's a real CTA" — honest convergence, keep its weight). Sites whose geometry/semantics diverge from `Button`'s pill tonal (toggle chips, `aria-pressed` cards, raw `color-mix` badges) converge on the TOKEN/opacity (standardize on /15 tint + on-token color-mix) without forcing the `Button` shape where it changes semantics.
  - **G3 — retry-button family** → converge the 6 divergent retries onto the shared **`Button`** (`variant="ghost" size="xs"` + `className="text-primary"` for the quiet inline retries per CodeCockpitPage:2904/2997 precedent; `variant="secondary"` for the filled one per CodeCockpitPage:471). NO new `RetryButton` primitive (would compete with `Button`).
  - **G4 — text-link idiom** → **extract a shared `<TextLink>` primitive** and migrate all 11 sites (`text-primary hover:underline`, with a size/icon-slot API covering leading-icon / trailing-icon / plain / the one real `<a>`). Owner chose the primitive over leave-as-idiom — fits the primitive-adoption ratchet, prevents future drift.
  - **G5 — capability picker** → **unify on `CapRow` everywhere** (the full-width row + checkbox + description + peek). Rewrite the code-plan `CapChip` surface (`CodePlanReview:391`) to use `CapRow` — hoist `CapRow` to a shared `ui/` (or shared plan-review) component consumed by both `LoopPlanReview` and `CodePlanReview`. **This moves pixels + rewrites the code-plan picker layout — explicitly owner-authorized.**
  - **G6 — ghost Cancel/Done** → converge onto **`Button variant="ghost"`**; the quiet surface-filled pill variants (`FilterMenu:83`, `TaskForm:161`) collapse into ghost.
  - **G7 — LoopCockpit Back-icon** (`LoopCockpitPage:720`) → converge onto **`SquareIconButton`** (neutral tone; size-6 → size-7 + hover:bg-surface-high, a legitimate pixel move).
  - **G8 — add-prerequisite** (`formControls:95`) → converge onto **`Button variant="secondary"`**.
  - **G9 — InlineError divergent strips** → **FOLD ALL 3 IN** (owner: maximum convergence, accept pixel moves): `ChatPage ErrorLine` (2633), `FilesSection` strip (351), AND the warn-toned animated `MoveErrorBanner` (`TasksListPage:428`) all converge onto **`InlineError`** — including **re-toning the warn banner to danger** and giving `InlineError` the API it needs (a `tone`? an `animated`? a non-dismissible mode + multi-line `items-start`). Update the primitive's docblock (which currently documents these as deliberately excluded) to reflect the fold-in.

### 2026-07-24 — cy51 DONE (G1 B′ token normalization — warm-up, batch cycle 1/9)
- **DONE:** Converged the last `*-error` off-token color spellings onto the dominant `*-danger` idiom. The B′ bug named in the ruling was `OllamaModelManager:88` `hover:text-error`; auditing the whole tree showed it was one of **6 identical `text-error` usages** in `web/src/pages/settings/` (`OllamaModelManager` L54/88/146/199, `ProjectionRulesPanel` L60, `SecurityPanel` L97/212). Normalized all 6 → `text-danger`. **Zero** `*-error` utility spellings now remain in `web/src/` (was 6; `*-danger` idiom count: 102).

Fixing only L88 while leaving 5 byte-identical siblings would manufacture a fresh inconsistency and violate clean-break; converging all 6 is the correct atomic unit. Stayed strictly within the danger color family (did not touch `text-warn`/`text-ok`/etc. — different semantic families, outside this ruling).

### 2026-07-24 — cy5 DONE (G4 text-link idiom — extract the `<TextLink>` primitive + migrate every hand-rolled `text-primary hover:underline` site — batch cycle 5/9)
- **Context:** the inline text-link idiom (`text-primary hover:underline`) was hand-rolled at ~16 call sites with drifting size classes, element types (`<button>` vs `<a>`), icon slots, and margins. G4's ruling: **extract a shared `<TextLink>` primitive** (owner chose the primitive over leave-as-idiom, per the primitive-adoption ratchet) with **a size + icon-slot API covering leading-icon / trailing-icon / plain / the one real `<a>`**. NOT a new visual language — the coral label + hover-underline is unchanged; this is the single-source consolidation of an existing idiom.
- **DONE (new primitive `ui/TextLink.tsx`):** minimal typed props following the `QuietButton` template — `children`, `href?` (+ `external?` → `target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer"` for off-app URLs; in-app hash links stay bare), `onClick?`, `icon?`/`iconPosition?` (`leading`|`trailing`, default leading)/`iconSize?`, `size?` (`xs`=`text-[0.75rem]` / `sm`=`text-[0.8125rem]` / `inherit`=no size class, the default in-sentence case), `disabled?`, `title?`, `className?` (rides layout extras like `ml-auto`/`mt-1.5`/`normal-case`). Renders a `<button type="button">` by default or an `<a href>` when `href` is set; opts into `inline-flex items-center gap-1` **only when an icon is present** so plain links flow inside running text without a baseline shift. Base class via the house `cx` joiner: `text-primary hover:underline disabled:opacity-50`. Docblock explains the idiom + every knob.
- **DONE (test `ui/TextLink.test.tsx`):** 7 cases in the `QuietButton.test.tsx` style — locks the coral/hover-underline/disabled-dim traits, the default `<button type="button">`, the `<a href>` path (+ bare, no target/rel for in-app), the external off-app safety attrs, the icon-row `inline-flex` opt-in, the `xs`/`sm`/`inherit` size-class mapping, and disabled+title forwarding + className merge. (A first draft of the size case rendered three `TextLink`s into the shared jsdom body and tripped `getByRole('button')`'s multiple-match — scoped each to its own `render().container` and it's green; not a primitive bug.)

### 2026-07-24 — cy6 DONE (G2 tonal / primary-fill convergence — the two faithful moves the ruling's geometry/semantics guardrail actually authorizes — batch cycle 6/9)
- **Context + the "~44" reconciliation (the substance of this cycle):** G2's ruling (line 480) reads as a large sweep — "the ~44 genuinely-tonal sites converge onto `Button variant="tonal"`" — but it carries its own explicit discriminator: **"where geometry matches"** and **"Sites whose geometry/semantics diverge from `Button`'s pill tonal (toggle chips, `aria-pressed` cards, raw `color-mix` badges) converge on the TOKEN/opacity … without forcing the `Button` shape where it changes semantics."** A fresh whole-tree audit (`grep` for every `bg-primary/NN` + `color-mix(var(--color-primary) …)` fill outside `ui/`, ~60 hits, each read in context) resolves the "~44" into **four distinct roles the ruling separates**, NOT 44 Button candidates:
  1. **Genuine tonal CTA with Button-matching geometry — 1 site.** `CodePlanReview:380` "Install" used the *exact* canonical idiom the `tonal` variant's docblock was written to absorb (`bg-primary/15 text-primary hover:bg-primary/25`), as a standalone clickable action hand-rolling its own `Loader2` spinner. This is a real primitive adoption → converted.
  2. **Owner-named promotion — 1 site.** `ProjectsSection:514` `/90` "New" split-button → the honest primary token (below).
  3. **Divergent-geometry clickable micro-pills — left on-token (per the carve-out).** The project-scope chips (`LoopCockpitPage:509`, `DesignCockpitPage:253`, `TasksListPage:515`) are **`h-5`/`h-6` pills sitting in `MetaPill` sibling rows**; `DagView:207` Approve is half of an **Approve/Deny pair** where Deny is `color-mix(--color-danger)` (no tonal-*danger* `Button` variant exists). Converting to `Button`'s smallest `h-7` pill would break meta-row height alignment, and splitting the Approve/Deny pair would manufacture a fresh inconsistency (the cy3 whole-row lesson). All are **already on-token** at the standard 16% `color-mix` — exactly the "converge on the token, don't force the shape" case.
  4. **Toggle chips / `aria-pressed` selection states / static badges / icon-wells / info-washes — left on-token (already converged).** The bulk (~40): `CapChip`/preset-chip toggles (`ScheduleForm:244`, `CodePlanReview:481`, settings `SearchPanel`/`ModelsPanel`/`MemoryPanel`/`DesignPanel`/`DiagnosticsPanel` on/off states), count badges (`NotificationsPage:84`, `LoopCockpitPage:1138`), step-number pills (`WorkflowForm`/`WorkflowDetail`), icon-wells (`AgentsListPage:191`, `SecurityPanel:40`), and 10% drag/info washes. These are **already** on the on-token idiom (`color-mix(var(--color-primary) …)` / `bg-primary/NN`), and their opacity spread (**10% wash → 14–16% chip/well → 18–20% emphasis badge**) is a **deliberate visual hierarchy** — flattening it to one value is a *redesign*, which the soul guardrail ("consistency, not redesign; bring outliers to the system") forbids. No outliers here; nothing to do.
- **DONE (CodePlanReview:380 Install → `Button variant="tonal"`):** replaced the raw `<button className="… bg-primary/15 … hover:bg-primary/25 …">{installing ? <Loader2 …/> : <Plus/>} {installing ? 'Installing…' : 'Install'}</button>` with `<Button variant="tonal" size="xs" className="shrink-0 gap-1 px-2 text-[0.75rem]" loading={!!installing[m.id]} onClick={() => onInstall(m)}><Plus size={11}/> Install</Button>`. Button's `loading` prop natively cross-fades the label under a centred spinner while preserving width — so the hand-rolled `Loader2`/label ternary is dropped (Loader2 import stays; still used at 184/293). **Default pill, not `shape="squircle"`:** an initial edit preserved the outlier's `rounded-md` via squircle, but a grep showed all 3 established `variant="tonal"` precedents (`CodeCockpitPage:1501/1935/3304`) render the **default pill** — squircle would be the only one in the tree, a fresh one-off. Corrected to the house default; `px-2`/`text-[0.75rem]`/`gap-1` retained via `className` to keep the dense in-row footprint. Raw-button count `10→9`.
- **DONE (ProjectsSection:514 "New" split-button → honest primary token):** owner ruling — "it's a real CTA … keep its weight." The trigger is a `Popover` render-prop receiving `(open, toggle)` with `aria-expanded={open}`, sitting in a `rounded-md` `HeaderControl` sibling row; `Button` accepts neither `aria-expanded` nor a non-pill row geometry, so forcing a `Button` pill here would **break the popover a11y contract and the control-row geometry** — the ruling's "don't force the shape where it changes semantics" applies to the promotion too. The faithful convergence is to **normalize the off-token `bg-primary/90` → the honest `bg-primary text-on-primary` token in place**, matching `Button variant="primary"`'s exact semantics (`bg-primary` idle, `hover:bg-primary-emphasis`). Class went `${open ? 'bg-primary text-on-primary' : 'bg-primary/90 text-on-primary hover:bg-primary'}` → `text-on-primary ${open ? 'bg-primary' : 'bg-primary hover:bg-primary-emphasis'}`. Raw-button count unchanged (still a raw `<button>` by necessity — the a11y trigger — just re-toned; correct).

### 2026-07-24 — cy12 DONE (S2 raw-input migration wave 3 — the model-provider settings text field converges onto `TextInput`)

The clean idiom is instead the **hand-rolled field constant that already IS a `TextInput` variant**: two files define `const inputCls = 'h-9 w-full rounded-md bg-surface-high px-3 text-[0.8125rem] … focus:ring-primary/50'` — **exactly** `TextInput size="md" surface="high"` (px-3 == px-m == 12px). Converging its plain-text adopters is pure de-duplication.
- **Scope (6 fields, byte-identical):**
  1. **ModelBackends** (`AddInstanceForm` "Instance name" + `EditInstanceForm` AWS region / AWS profile / Endpoint / Default model) — 5 plain `className={inputCls}` + `aria-label` text `<input>`s → `<TextInput size="md" surface="high" ariaLabel=…>`. The `onChange={(e)=>setX(e.target.value)}` wrappers collapse to the primitive's value-first `onChange={setX}`.
  2. **MultiInstanceCard** ("Instance name", the one field using the imported `inputCls + ' mb-2'`) → `<TextInput size="md" surface="high" ariaLabel="Instance name">` in a `mb-2` wrapper div (the primitive is `w-full`; the margin rides the wrapper). Dropped the now-unused `inputCls` import (kept `SchemaField`/`schemaDefaults`).

- **Pixel-identity (no visual move):** `md` = `h-9 text-[0.8125rem]`, `INPUT_BASE` = the exact `rounded-md` + `focus:ring-2 focus:ring-inset focus:ring-primary/50` + tones, `surface="high"` = `bg-surface-high`, inline `px-m` == the prior `px-3` (12px). Rendered chrome is byte-for-byte the prior field. No `e2e/routes.ts` baseline moves (these forms live behind the Providers → Model-providers section, not a captured shell), none regenerated.

### 2026-07-24 — cy13 DONE (S3 a11y parity — the ten non-default color schemes reach WCAG AA in light mode, closing the cy15 DEVIATION; a permanent per-scheme contrast guard replaces the default-only axe rail)
- **Why this, why now (conformance, not taste — so it's in-scope autonomously):** cy15 recorded a standing DEVIATION — the coral **default** scheme was AA-corrected and is enforced live by `e2e/a11y.spec.ts` (axe), but **axe only ever exercises the active/default scheme**, so the other ten curated schemes (`schemes.ts`) still carried **pre-AA light-mode accents** that no rail caught. PRODUCT.md mandates WCAG 2.1 AA (owner-confirmed, owner-task-3), so a shipped scheme below 4.5:1 is a **conformance defect**, not a taste call. Fixing it needs no owner gate. Soul-guardrail honored: this is **not a redesign** — every accent stays the same recognizable hue, darkened only enough to be legible on white; dark mode (already AA) is untouched; no new visual language, no page changes.
- **Measurement (WCAG 2.1 relative-luminance, 4.5:1 small-text):** measured all 11 schemes in the two contexts each light accent actually bears text/UI — `primary` as a filled control (onPrimary over primary) **and** as accent text on the white surface, `primaryEmphasis` as the hover fill, `info` as accent text on white. **6 schemes failed** the light bar (dark passed everywhere — coral, the AA-verified default, sits at 5.9:1 on `surface-container`, so dark was already clean, matching cy15): honey (primary 3.38, emphasis 4.49), jade (3.74), ember (4.17), forest (primary 3.57, info 4.0), rose (4.24), amber (primary 3.25, emphasis 4.27, info 3.25). `lavender`/`ocean`/`slate`/`mono`/`coral` already passed → untouched.
- **Correction (hue-preserving, matches coral's headroom):** darkened only the failing **light** values (HSL-darken at constant hue/sat) to land at coral's ~4.83:1 fill/text headroom, keeping `emphasis` darker than `primary` (hover-darkening invariant): honey `#c17d18→#9d6614` / emph `#a96812→#985e10`; jade `#0d9488→#0b7f75`; ember `#d1543a→#c5482e`; forest `#1f9b58→#1a824a` / info `#0a8f80→#098073`; rose `#d8407e→#d22b6f`; amber `#cf7a23→#a5611c` / emph `#b8651a→#a15817` / info `#cf7a23→#a5611c`. **Every dark value, and all decorative glow/gradient stops, stay exactly as-is** — same coral precedent (glow/gradient are non-text-bearing, keep the brighter accent). The `scheme()` builder maps `--grad-2` light to `primary[1]`, so each fixed scheme's light gradient now rides its AA primary — exactly as coral already does. **After fix: all 11 schemes pass** (min 4.57:1).
- **Structural close of the cy15 hole — new permanent guard `web/src/design/schemeContrast.test.ts`:** iterates **every** `SCHEMES` entry (not just the default) and asserts each one's `primary`/`primaryEmphasis`/`info` meets ≥4.5:1 in both modes — light: filled-control, accent-text-on-white, emphasis-hover; dark: primary/info as accent text on `--color-surface-container` (read live from `tokens.css` so a token retint can't silently drift the guard; `surface-highest` deliberately NOT the reference — even the AA default coral is 4.4:1 there, so it isn't a text-bearing ground). **67 assertions (11×6 + set check), all green.** A new scheme or a regressed edit that dips below AA now turns the build red — the design-side complement to the runtime axe rail, and it covers schemes axe never activates. **This retires the cy15 DEVIATION.**
- **Validation (drove the running dev gateway, not just the source):** bundled Playwright against `127.0.0.1:10000` — forced `.light`, applied each scheme's light color set to `document.documentElement` exactly as `appearance.tsx`'s `applyScheme` does (`root.style.setProperty` per accent var), read the **computed** CSS vars back out, and measured live DOM contrast: **all 11 pass, zero console errors.** Because the **default coral is unchanged**, no `e2e/routes.ts` route-shell baseline moves (they're captured in the default scheme) — none regenerated, per "regen only where pixels legitimately move." `tsc --noEmit` clean; full vitest **217/217** across 25 files (was 150 pre-cy13's earlier count; +67 from the new guard, plus suites that grew in interim cycles); `npm run build` clean.
- **No ratchet delta:** this cycle touches color-token *values* only — no raw `<button>`/`<input>`/`<select>` count changes, so `primitiveAdoption.baseline.json` (285/151/0/7) and `consistency-audit.json` are untouched (the audit's only diff on a suite run is its `generatedAt` timestamp — reverted to keep the commit focused).
- **NEXT:** S3 interaction/responsive parity tail (T3.3) and the remaining `rawInput`/`rawButton` drawdown where a byte-identical idiom exists. Environment-blocked tail stays honestly undone: the **authenticated axe-per-route CI gate** (T3.2/T3.4) and the **V3 full-app walkthrough** need a seeded, authenticated, per-route CI harness this autonomous loop can't stand up — recorded, not faked. Chaining per the autonomous-loop-continuity directive.

### 2026-07-24 — cy14 DONE (S2 raw-button wave — the reveal-X row-delete family in `formControls.tsx` converges onto `SquareIconButton tone="danger"`; owner-authorized under the full-authority grant)
- **Why in-scope now:** cy36's deferral list (plan §"Not this cycle") flagged the **`RowActionButton` family** as owner-gated because converging it "moves pixels" and its variants (toggle / danger-reveal / danger-always) seemed to need a new `tone`/`active` API. Two things resolve that: (1) the owner's **full-authority taste grant** authorizes the pixel move; (2) the API the plan wanted **already shipped** — `SquareIconButton` carries both `tone="danger"` (idle ink-low → hover red glyph, **no fill**) and `on` (active tint), and the canonical reveal-danger row-action **already ships** at `CodeSection.tsx:384` (`SquareIconButton icon={Trash2} tone="danger"` + reveal on `className`). So this is no longer "design a primitive" — it's converging raw adopters onto the shape that already exists. Soul-guardrail honored: consolidation onto a shipped pattern, not a redesign.
- **Scope (3 byte-adjacent sites, one file — the tightest sub-cluster of the family):** `pages/tasks/formControls.tsx` — the three identical reveal-X delete buttons (`shrink-0 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 text-on-surface-low hover:text-danger transition-opacity` + `<X size={14}>`): `DependencyEditor` prerequisite-remove (was **unnamed** — bare `<X>`, no `aria-label`), `ChecklistEditor` `rowInner` remove, `NotesEditor` note-remove → `<SquareIconButton icon={X} tone="danger" label=… onClick=… className="shrink-0 opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" />`. Added the `SquareIconButton` import; `X` retained (now the `icon={X}` prop).
- **Faithfulness (what moves, what doesn't):** the danger tone renders `text-on-surface-low hover:text-danger` with **no fill** — byte-identical hover treatment to the raw buttons. ### 2026-07-24 — cy16 DONE (S2 raw-input migration wave 4 — the dense modal/side-panel `<textarea>` cluster converges onto `TextArea`, which grows a lockstep `size` axis; owner-authorized under the full-authority grant)
- **Why in-scope now:** rotates from the (now-converged) raw-button idiom back to the S2 raw-**input** drawdown flagged in cy15's NEXT. Earlier cycles (3/5/14) established there is **no byte-identical raw-`<input>` cluster** left (the survivors hardcode `flex-1`/`px-2`/`text-on-surface-var`/custom `onBlur` — a forced `TextInput` migration would move pixels or drop behavior, so it is correctly NOT forced). The faithful cluster that DOES exist is two **dense `<textarea>`** call-sites sharing chrome byte-for-byte with each other; per the plan's explicit lockstep rule ("structural props grown with the first real adopter"), **two** real adopters needing the dense size is the exact trigger to give `TextArea` its `size` axis. Codification onto the shipped primitive, not a redesign; soul-guardrail honored.
- **Primitive growth (lockstep, byte-identical by construction):** `ui/forms.tsx` `TextArea` gains `size?: FieldSize` (default `'lg'`) + `autoFocus?: boolean`, backed by a new `TEXTAREA_TEXT` record (`sm/md → text-[0.8125rem]`, `lg → text-[0.9375rem]` — the same on-ramp type steps as `FIELD_SIZE`, minus the height rung since a textarea sizes from `rows`). The `mono` branch is unchanged: it still appends `font-mono text-[0.8125rem]` **after** the size text, so every existing (default-`lg`) and mono adopter renders token-for-token as before — the migration moves zero pixels for anyone not opting into `sm`. The stale "TextArea will adopt the scale later" comment is replaced with the accurate "DateInput / Select keep the prior fixed chrome" note (they remain un-grown — no adopter yet).
- **Scope (2 sites, 2 files — the dense-textarea cluster):** (1) `pages/agents/AgentDetail.tsx:152` routing-notes editor (`rows={3}`, `aria-label="Routing notes"`); (2) `pages/files/comments/CommentLayer.tsx:285` submit-comments modal instructions (`autoFocus`, `rows={4}`). Both were raw `<textarea className="w-full resize-y rounded-md bg-surface-container px-3 py-2 text-[0.8125rem] …focus:ring-2 focus:ring-inset focus:ring-primary/50">` → `<TextArea size="sm" ariaLabel=… [autoFocus] rows=…>`. Added the `TextArea` import to each.
- **Faithfulness (what moves, what doesn't):** `TextArea size="sm"` renders `w-full rounded-md bg-surface-container px-m py-2 text-on-surface text-[0.8125rem] placeholder:text-on-surface-low outline-none resize-y focus:ring-2 focus:ring-inset focus:ring-primary/50` — the raw markup's chrome exactly, with **one token-cleanup delta**: hardcoded `px-3` → the scale-responsive `px-m` (`calc(12px * var(--space-scale))`, ==12px==`px-3` at default scale, but now honors the density scale like every other form primitive). All other tokens byte-identical. **a11y gain (CommentLayer):** the modal textarea previously had **no accessible name** (only a sibling visual `<label>`, unassociated) — it now carries `ariaLabel="Additional instructions (optional)"` matching that label. AgentDetail already had `aria-label="Routing notes"`, preserved verbatim.
- **Deliberately NOT migrated (correctly stay raw — faithfulness triage):** the **`resize-none` inline composers** in the same CommentLayer file (`:104` add-comment popover, `:258` edit-comment card) — they carry bespoke `resize-none` + custom `onKeyDown` (Enter-saves / Esc-cancels) + non-container surfaces (`bg-surface-container`/`bg-surface-high`), so a faithful migration would need `resize`/`onKeyDown` props grown on the primitive first (a separate lockstep step, not this cluster). The **mono prompt textareas** (`PromptEditFields`/`PromptForm`/`SnippetForm`) already ride `TextArea mono` (default `lg` — untouched, still byte-identical). No raw-`<input>` migrated this cycle (no faithful cluster; see Why).
- **Ratchet: `rawInput` 151 → 149 (−2), committed same cycle.** Live count re-measured via the reporter's own `scanDrift()` (the scanner folds `<textarea>`/`<select>` into the `raw-input` count); two files each −1 (AgentDetail 1→0, CommentLayer 3→2 — its two `resize-none` composers correctly remain). `primitiveAdoption.baseline.json` `rawInput: 151 → 149`; `rawButton` (278)/`rawDialog` (0)/`inlineFontWeight` (7) untouched. ### 2026-07-24 — cy18 DONE (assessment/validation cycle — the autonomous-completable convergence lanes are code-verified DRAINED; no source change, and manufacturing a marginal one would violate the soul guardrail)
- **Why a no-code cycle is the honest move (not a stop, not a manufactured edit):** the autonomous-loop-continuity directive says don't stop while authorized work remains — but the soul guardrail ("consistency, not redesign; add a shared primitive *only when the system genuinely lacks a pattern*") forbids inventing work. cy14→cy17 drained every *byte-identical* convergence cluster; this cycle's job was to **prove, against real code (E1 discipline, not prior-cycle memory), that the remaining candidates are genuinely non-convergeable** so a future session doesn't blindly re-scan — and to record the residual tail's true blocker. All four candidate lanes were re-inspected at source this cycle; each fails the guardrail for a *distinct, verified* reason, not a blanket stop.
- **Lane 1 — top raw-button offender re-inspected (`CodeCockpitPage`, 21 raw `<button>`):** read the class signature of all 21. **Zero byte-identical cluster.** Each is bespoke context-chrome — a rename-title trigger (`:610`, `data-type="title-l"` text button), dynamic-tone "Respond" chip (`:754`, inline `style` tone), breadcrumb link (`:912`), file-tree/commit/file rows (`:1151/2364/2385`, full-width `text-left` list rows), a view-tab toggle (`:1351`, `on`-state inline style), an `aria-pressed` autopilot toggle (`:1494`), a collapsible section header (`:1589`, rotating chevron), a task row (`:1677`), a blocker chip (`:1704`), a "show earlier cycles" text link (`:1881`), panel-reopen buttons (`:2750/2780`). None map onto `Button`/`IconButton`/`SquareIconButton` without moving pixels **and** changing semantics — that is the harness-gated *redesign* migration the guardrail forbids doing as a "consistency" pass, not a byte-identical convergence.
- **Lane 2 — the `[ ] Error state` primitive (patterns.md open item) re-evaluated at source:** read both load-error blocks. They **genuinely diverge across 6 dimensions**: `CodeSection:305` = page-level `gap-l py-2xl`, `AlertTriangle` **danger** 32px, `headline-s`, hint `text-[0.9375rem]`, `size="sm"` Retry; `CodeCockpitPage:465` = constrained `max-w-[360px] gap-3 px-6` inside `<Centered>`, `AlertTriangle` **warn** 28px, `title-m`, hint `text-[0.8125rem]`, `variant="secondary"` "Try again" that's **auto-retrying** (`— retrying…`). An `ErrorState` primitive would need a knob per difference and would gain exactly **one** faithful adopter — **premature abstraction with a single real user**, nothing like the 5-copy (`SquareIconButton`) / ~200-copy (`TextField`) bar that justified those extractions. Correctly stays an open follow-on, NOT built.
- **Lane 3 — cy15's deferred filled/labeled raw-button family re-inspected:** `ProviderCard:131/136/140`'s three action pills (Test/Connect/Disconnect) are byte-identical *to each other* but are **not a faithful `Button` adopter** — verified against `ui/Button.tsx`: they render `rounded-md bg-surface-high px-2 py-1 text-[0.75rem]` with **per-action text-color hovers** (`hover:text-on-surface`/`hover:text-danger`/`hover:text-primary`) and an **inline** spinner swap, whereas `Button` is `rounded-pill`, min `h-7`, smallest text `0.8125rem`, hovers by changing **background** (`secondary` = `hover:bg-surface-highest`), and `loading` cross-fades the whole label. Converging would flip radius + grow text + invert the hover model + drop the tint — a control redesign. A tree-wide scan for the dense labeled-chip signature (`bg-surface-high` + `px-2 py-1` + `text-[0.75rem]`) returned **11 hits, but only ProviderCard's 3 share the full idiom** — the rest are a badge `<span>` (VoicePanel:146), a hover-overlay strip (MemoryPanel:319), tri-state toggles (ProjectsSection:508, CodeCockpitPage:1496), and a `<select>` (CodePlanReview:545). **3 same-file copies is below the 5-copy missing-primitive bar** → a `LabeledChipButton` primitive would be premature abstraction; left raw.
- **Lane 4 — neutral-toggle (`VoicePanel:299`) re-confirmed:** the enable/disable term button (`text-on-surface-low hover:text-on-surface`, no fill) converging to `SquareIconButton` would *add* the primitive's `hover:bg-surface-high` fill — a hover-state pixel change, i.e. its own deliberate cycle, not a faithful drop-in. Stays raw (matches cy15's recorded triage).
- **T3.3 parity — re-verified holding (prior-cycle live evidence, no new pixels this cycle):** the autonomous-checkable T3.3 surface already holds from the cy13→cy17 live runs — dark/light contrast (all 11 schemes ≥4.5:1 AA, guarded permanently by `schemeContrast.test.ts`), and the earlier phone-width sweep found **0 horizontal-overflow routes at 375px**. No source moved this cycle, so nothing re-drove; the standing guards (axe rail + `schemeContrast.test.ts`) remain the live enforcement.
- **No ratchet delta, no source change (validation-only cycle, like a V-task):** `primitiveAdoption.baseline.json` stays `{ rawButton: 278, rawInput: 149, rawDialog: 0, inlineFontWeight: 7 }`; `consistency-audit.json` untouched (no suite re-run needed — no code moved); `tokenLint.allowlist.json` remains its single `WidgetFrame` entry; the 6 residual `driftHits` are all legitimately dynamic (guardrail-protected, not convergeable). Tree left clean.
- **Honest state of the plan — this is the autonomous-completable FLOOR:** every convergence a fully-autonomous loop can land *faithfully* (byte-identical extraction, or an outlier normalized onto a shipped primitive) is done. The residual tail is **not** more of the same work — it is three categories that each need something this loop cannot supply: (1) **owner/design decisions** — the `ErrorState` primitive (a new-primitive call with one adopter), and the harness-gated `CodeCockpitPage`-class raw-button *redesign* migration (moves pixels + changes control semantics, which is the redesign the guardrail gates); (2) **primitive-growth prerequisites** — the `resize`/`onKeyDown` inline-composer textareas need those props grown on `TextArea` first (a lockstep step with no current second adopter to trigger it faithfully); (3) **environment-blocked** — the authenticated axe-per-route CI gate (T3.2/T3.4) and the V3 full-app walkthrough need a seeded, authenticated, per-route CI harness this loop can't stand up. **NEXT: surface this floor to the owner rather than manufacture marginal cycles** — continuing to invent sub-5-copy "clusters" or single-adopter primitives would be exactly the redesign-creep the soul guardrail exists to prevent. Recorded honestly, not faked.

### 2026-08-18 — DSC-12 DONE (the deferred owner/design tail, taken under granted taste-call authority: the load-error twin folded, all 21 CodeCockpitPage raw buttons classified — 7 migrated, 14 excluded with reasons)

- **Why this became completable — cy18's Lane 1 and Lane 2 conclusions were right in July and are stale now.** cy18 recorded that an `ErrorState` primitive "would gain exactly **one** faithful adopter — premature abstraction", and that of the cockpit's 21 raw buttons "**Zero** byte-identical cluster... None map onto `Button`/`IconButton`/`SquareIconButton` without moving pixels **and** changing semantics". Both were honest readings of the system *as it then was*. The system has since grown the exact patterns those readings were missing, which is what unblocked this atom: `LoadError({what, error, onRetry})` now lives in `ui/ListScaffold.tsx` **beside `EmptyState`** with **13 adopters** and its own rail (`ui/loadErrorState.test.tsx`) — so clause 1 was never "extract ErrorState", it was "fold the twin into the primitive that got built anyway"; `Button` gained a **`size="xs"`** tier whose own comment says it is for "dense in-panel chrome (**cockpit banners**, error retries, inline strips) — *the tier whose absence made pages hand-roll `<button>`s*"; `Button` gained a **`tonal`** variant documented as "the pattern pages kept hand-rolling as `bg-primary/15 text-primary hover:bg-primary/25`"; and `TextLink` shipped as the single source for the text-affordance idiom, naming **"Show more"** as a canonical `size="sm"` use. The deferred tail did not need a redesign decision so much as it needed the primitives to arrive first.
- **Clause 1a — the page-level twin (`CodeCockpitPage:474`) folded onto `LoadError`.** The bespoke block was a `max-w-[360px]` column with a **warn**-toned 28px `AlertTriangle`, a `title-m` "Couldn't load this project", the message with a `— retrying…` suffix, and a `secondary` "Try again". Right information, wrong tone (a failed load is danger, not caution), wrong type scale — and, because it was hand-rolled instead of reaching for the primitive, **no `role="alert"`**. That is the sharpest part of the finding: the surface a user reaches from *every* project row was the one silent member of the family this rail exists to guard, while `CodeSection` — the list page one click above it — had been an adopter for cycles. `loadErr` now holds the **rejection** rather than a pre-flattened string, so the server's own message reaches the primitive.
  - **Taste call, recorded as a loss:** the fold **drops the `— retrying…` suffix**. It was truthful (`useVisiblePoll` re-fetches every 4s while `project === null && loadErr`) but it is a per-caller sentence, and growing `LoadError` a prop for one site is exactly the "manufactured" API the atom warns against. The auto-retry still runs; the Retry button still recovers manually. Cheaper to lose one clause than to widen a 13-adopter primitive for it.
  - **Copy check:** `what="project"` renders "Couldn't load your project". The primitive's *fallback* sentence is written for plurals ("Your ${what} are safe"), so the capture keeps the existing `|| 'Could not load this project'` default — the fallback is unreachable rather than ungrammatical.
- **Clause 1b — `CodeCockpitPage:2938` is a DIFFERENT surface and takes a different primitive.** The mid-body commit-diff failure was `<span style={{ color: 'var(--color-danger)' }}>Couldn't load this commit.</span>` beside a ghost-accent retry, inside a narrow panel. It is **not** a `LoadError` (a 32px icon + `headline-s` + 420px paragraph would swamp the panel) and **not** an `InlineError` — `ui/forms.tsx` already draws that line: `InlineError` is "a tinted, dismissible strip **ABOVE a body**", `FieldError` is "the terse line that belongs to one control or one action". This is the second. It also happens to be byte-for-byte the shape `FieldError` was extracted to replace ("the shape 30 call sites had hand-rolled as `<p className="text-danger text-[0.8125rem]">{err}</p>`"), so the convergence is exact and the panel gains its **first live region** — same class of defect as 1a, one panel deeper.
- **Clause 2 — all 21 raw `<button>`s classified. 7 migrated, 14 deliberately not.** The test of a migration was: *does a shipped primitive already own this pattern, such that the raw element is the accident?* Where the answer was no, the honest outcome is an exclusion, not a conversion — forcing those would be the page rewrite the soul guardrail forbids.
  - **MIGRATED (7).**
    - `:1526` autopilot toggle → **`Button variant={autopilot ? 'tonal' : 'ghost'} size="xs" ariaPressed disabled`**. The strongest case in the file, and `design/accentChip.test.ts` had already found it: its allowlist comment named "`Button`'s `tonal` variant (14 callers) **and** the Code cockpit's autopilot toggle" as the same `bg-primary/15 → hover:bg-primary/25` control, spelled twice. The migration collapses that pair, and `CLASS_TINT_ALLOWED` **shrinks from two entries to one** (its own staleness check demanded this, and went red until I did it). Stated exactly: the 3.92→3.39 contrast debt **did not improve — it stopped being duplicated**, and now has one owner to fix when the token set gains a container hover shade.

Three copies of one shape, and `size="xs"`'s own doc names this file's class of chrome as its reason for existing. Pixels move: `rounded-md` → `rounded-pill`, and `:2870` goes 12px → 13px text. Accepted deliberately — the cockpit *already* renders a pill `<Button variant="ghost" size="xs">Dismiss</Button>` two lines from a raw sibling (`:772`), so pill is the in-file majority, not a new language.
    - `:2809` clean-tab close X → **`IconButton size={18} iconSize={12}`**, whose `iconSize` doc invites exactly this ("Dense toolbars/inline chrome use smaller glyphs (12–16)... the prop lets those sites adopt the primitive instead of hand-rolling a `<button>`"). Net gain: a real hit area where there was only a bare 12px glyph.
    - `:1913` "↑ N earlier cycles" and `:3389` "+N more" → **`TextLink size="xs" ink="emphasis"`**, the capped-list disclosure the system already ships (live sibling: `ScheduleDetail.tsx:336` `Show more (N more)`). `ink="emphasis"` rather than the default, per `TextLink`'s own measured table: plain `primary` fails AA on `surface-low`/`surface-high` in 6–10 of the 12 schemes, and these sit on panel grounds, not `--color-surface`.
